import tailwindcss from '@tailwindcss/vite';
import react from '@vitejs/plugin-react';
import path from 'path';
import {defineConfig} from 'vite';
import {VitePWA} from 'vite-plugin-pwa';

export default defineConfig(() => {
  return {
    plugins: [
      react(),
      tailwindcss(),
      VitePWA({
        registerType: 'autoUpdate',
        includeAssets: ['icon.svg', 'pwa-192x192.png', 'pwa-512x512.png', 'pwa-maskable-512x512.png', 'apple-touch-icon.png'],
        manifest: {
          id: '/',
          name: 'Bóveda Segura - Fotos, Videos y Apps',
          short_name: 'Mi Bóveda',
          description: 'Bóveda privada y cifrada con AES-GCM para almacenar fotos, videos y aplicaciones localmente.',
          theme_color: '#090d16',
          background_color: '#090d16',
          display: 'standalone',
          orientation: 'portrait',
          start_url: '/',
          scope: '/',
          icons: [
            {
              src: '/pwa-192x192.png',
              sizes: '192x192',
              type: 'image/png',
              purpose: 'any',
            },
            {
              src: '/pwa-512x512.png',
              sizes: '512x512',
              type: 'image/png',
              purpose: 'any',
            },
            {
              src: '/pwa-maskable-512x512.png',
              sizes: '512x512',
              type: 'image/png',
              purpose: 'maskable',
            },
            {
              src: '/icon.svg',
              sizes: 'any',
              type: 'image/svg+xml',
              purpose: 'any',
            },
          ],
        },
        devOptions: {
          enabled: true,
        },
      }),
    ],
    resolve: {
      alias: {
        '@': path.resolve(__dirname, '.'),
      },
    },
    server: {
      // HMR is disabled in AI Studio via DISABLE_HMR env var.
      // Do not modify—file watching is disabled to prevent flickering during agent edits.
      hmr: process.env.DISABLE_HMR !== 'true',
      // Disable file watching when DISABLE_HMR is true to save CPU during agent edits.
      watch: process.env.DISABLE_HMR === 'true' ? null : {},
    },
  };
});

import type { CapacitorConfig } from '@capacitor/cli';

const config: CapacitorConfig = {
  appId: 'com.calculadora.boveda.privada',
  appName: 'Mi Bóveda',
  webDir: 'dist',
  server: {
    androidScheme: 'https',
  },
  android: {
    allowMixedContent: false,
    captureInput: true,
  },
};

export default config;

================================================================================
INICIO DE ARCHIVO: src/App.tsx
================================================================================

/**
 * @license
 * SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
 */

import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef, useCallback } from 'react';
import { Shield } from 'lucide-react';
import { App as CapacitorApp } from '@capacitor/app';
import { SetupVaultView } from './components/SetupVaultView';
import { LockVaultView } from './components/LockVaultView';
import { MainVaultView } from './components/MainVaultView';
import { SettingsModal } from './components/SettingsModal';
import { ToastContainer, ToastData } from './components/Toast';
import { openVaultDatabase, clearVaultDatabase } from './db/vaultDb';
import { initScreenshotBlocker, setScreenshotBlockToastCallback } from './services/screenshotBlockService';
import { initBackButtonListener } from './services/backButtonService';
import { updateService, UpdateCheckResult } from './services/updateService';
import { UpdateNotificationBanner, UpdateNotificationModal } from './components/UpdateNotificationModal';
import { CURRENT_APP_VERSION } from './version';

export default function App() {
  const [vaultStatus, setVaultStatus] = useState<'checking' | 'setup' | 'locked' | 'unlocked'>('checking');
  const [masterKey, setMasterKey] = useState<CryptoKey | null>(null);
  const [sessionPin, setSessionPin] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [toasts, setToasts] = useState<ToastData[]>([]);
  const [isSettingsOpen, setIsSettingsOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isPrivacyBlurActive, setIsPrivacyBlurActive] = useState(false);

  // Settings
  const [autoLockMinutes, setAutoLockMinutes] = useState<number>(() => {
    const saved = localStorage.getItem('vault_autolock_mins');
    const val = Number(saved);
    return saved !== null && !isNaN(val) ? val : 5;
  });

  const [lockOnTabSwitch, setLockOnTabSwitch] = useState<boolean>(() => {
    const saved = localStorage.getItem('vault_lock_on_tab_switch');
    return saved !== null ? saved === 'true' : true;
  });

  const [vaultRefreshTrigger, setVaultRefreshTrigger] = useState<number>(0);

  // Sistema de Actualizaciones
  const [pendingUpdateInfo, setPendingUpdateInfo] = useState<UpdateCheckResult | null>(null);
  const [showUpdateBanner, setShowUpdateBanner] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [showUpdateModal, setShowUpdateModal] = useState<boolean>(false);

  const inactivityTimerRef = useRef<number | null>(null);

  // Toast Helper
  const showToast = useCallback((message: string, type: 'info' | 'success' | 'alert' | 'error' = 'info') => {
    const id = `${Date.now()}_${Math.random()}`;
    setToasts((prev) => [...prev, { id, message, type }]);

    setTimeout(() => {
      setToasts((prev) => prev.filter((t) => t.id !== id));
    }, 3200);
  }, []);

  // Lock Vault Action
  const lockVault = useCallback(() => {
    // Strictly forbid locking while picking files or running backup operations
    if ((window as any).__isPickingFile || (window as any).__isProcessingBackup) {
      return;
    }
    try {
      sessionStorage.removeItem('vault_session_pin');
    } catch {}
    setMasterKey(null);
    setSessionPin(null);
    setVaultStatus('locked');
    setIsSettingsOpen(false);
  }, []);

  // Memoized unlock handler with extended post-unlock immunity grace period (20 seconds)
  const handleUnlock = useCallback((key: CryptoKey, pin?: string) => {
    const now = Date.now();
    (window as any).__lastUnlockTime = now;
    (window as any).__lastBiometricUnlockTime = now;
    setMasterKey(key);
    if (pin) {
      setSessionPin(pin);
      try {
        sessionStorage.setItem('vault_session_pin', pin);
      } catch {}
    }
    setVaultStatus('unlocked');
  }, []);

  // Android Back navigation for settings
  const isSettingsOpenRef = useRef(isSettingsOpen);
  isSettingsOpenRef.current = isSettingsOpen;
  const wasSettingsOpenRef = useRef(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isSettingsOpen && !wasSettingsOpenRef.current) {
      window.history.pushState({ appOverlay: 'settings' }, '');
      wasSettingsOpenRef.current = true;
    } else if (!isSettingsOpen && wasSettingsOpenRef.current) {
      wasSettingsOpenRef.current = false;
      if (window.history.state?.appOverlay === 'settings') {
        window.history.back();
      }
    }
  }, [isSettingsOpen]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const handlePopState = () => {
      if (isSettingsOpenRef.current) {
        wasSettingsOpenRef.current = false;
        setIsSettingsOpen(false);
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener('popstate', handlePopState);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('popstate', handlePopState);
  }, []);

  // Check initial vault existence on mount
  useEffect(() => {
    const init = async () => {
      try {
        await openVaultDatabase();
        const isInit = localStorage.getItem('vault_initialized');
        const saltHex = localStorage.getItem('vault_salt');
        const testIvHex = localStorage.getItem('vault_test_iv');
        const testCipherHex = localStorage.getItem('vault_test_cipher');

        if (isInit === 'true' && saltHex && testIvHex && testCipherHex) {
          // Siempre requerir PIN o Huella al abrir o reabrir la aplicación
          setVaultStatus('locked');
        } else {
          setVaultStatus('setup');
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Initialization error:', err);
        setVaultStatus('setup');
      }
    };

    init();
    initScreenshotBlocker();
    setScreenshotBlockToastCallback(showToast);
    initBackButtonListener();
  }, [showToast]);

  // Root back button press notice
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleRootBack = () => {
      showToast('Presiona o desliza atrás de nuevo para salir', 'info');
    };
    const handleRootExit = () => {
      showToast('Saliendo de la bóveda...', 'info');
    };
    window.addEventListener('vault_root_back_press', handleRootBack);
    window.addEventListener('vault_root_exit_allowed', handleRootExit);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('vault_root_back_press', handleRootBack);
      window.removeEventListener('vault_root_exit_allowed', handleRootExit);
    };
  }, [showToast]);

  // Detección automática de actualizaciones al entrar a la aplicación
  useEffect(() => {
    // Si la app acaba de actualizarse, mostrar saludo de confirmación
    if (sessionStorage.getItem('vault_just_updated') === 'true') {
      sessionStorage.removeItem('vault_just_updated');
      setTimeout(() => {
        showToast(`¡Bóveda actualizada con éxito a la versión v${CURRENT_APP_VERSION}!`, 'success');
      }, 700);
    }

    if (!updateService.isAutoCheckEnabled()) return;

    // Comprobar actualizaciones tras 1.5s para no competir con el arranque inicial
    const timer = setTimeout(async () => {
      try {
        const res = await updateService.checkForUpdates();
        if (res.hasUpdate) {
          setPendingUpdateInfo(res);
          if (!updateService.isUpdateDismissed(res.latestVersion)) {
            setShowUpdateBanner(true);
          }
        }
      } catch (e) {
        console.warn('Error al verificar actualizaciones al entrar:', e);
      }
    }, 1500);

    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [showToast]);

  // Suscribirse a eventos de actualización
  useEffect(() => {
    return updateService.subscribe((res) => {
      if (res.hasUpdate) {
        setPendingUpdateInfo(res);
        if (!updateService.isUpdateDismissed(res.latestVersion)) {
          setShowUpdateBanner(true);
        }
      }
    });
  }, []);

  // App privacy blur shield and immediate locking when minimizing or switching apps
  useEffect(() => {
    let appStateHandle: any = null;

    const isUserPickingFiles = () => {
      if ((window as any).__isPickingFile) return true;
      const startTime = (window as any).__filePickingStartTime || 0;
      if (startTime > 0 && Date.now() - startTime < 180000) return true; // 3 min grace window while picking files
      return false;
    };

    const handleMinimizeOrBackground = () => {
      if (isUserPickingFiles() || (window as any).__isProcessingBackup) {
        return;
      }
      lockVault();
    };

    if (CapacitorApp && typeof CapacitorApp.addListener === 'function') {
      try {
        CapacitorApp.addListener('appStateChange', (state) => {
          if (!isUserPickingFiles()) {
            setIsPrivacyBlurActive(!state.isActive);
          }
          if (!state.isActive) {
            handleMinimizeOrBackground();
          } else {
            window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('vault_app_resumed'));
          }
        }).then((h) => {
          appStateHandle = h;
        }).catch(() => {});
      } catch {}
    }

    const handleDocVisibility = () => {
      const isHidden = document.visibilityState === 'hidden';
      if (!isUserPickingFiles()) {
        setIsPrivacyBlurActive(isHidden);
      }
      if (isHidden) {
        handleMinimizeOrBackground();
      } else {
        window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('vault_app_resumed'));
      }
    };

    const handleWindowBlur = () => {
      if (!isUserPickingFiles()) {
        setIsPrivacyBlurActive(true);
      }
      handleMinimizeOrBackground();
    };

    const handleWindowFocus = () => {
      setIsPrivacyBlurActive(false);
      window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('vault_app_resumed'));
    };

    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', handleDocVisibility);
    window.addEventListener('blur', handleWindowBlur);
    window.addEventListener('focus', handleWindowFocus);

    return () => {
      if (appStateHandle && typeof appStateHandle.remove === 'function') {
        appStateHandle.remove();
      }
      document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', handleDocVisibility);
      window.removeEventListener('blur', handleWindowBlur);
      window.removeEventListener('focus', handleWindowFocus);
    };
  }, [lockVault]);

  // Inactivity auto-lock
  useEffect(() => {
    const mins = Number(autoLockMinutes);
    if (vaultStatus !== 'unlocked' || isNaN(mins) || mins <= 0) {
      if (inactivityTimerRef.current) {
        window.clearTimeout(inactivityTimerRef.current);
        inactivityTimerRef.current = null;
      }
      return;
    }

    const resetTimer = () => {
      if (inactivityTimerRef.current) {
        window.clearTimeout(inactivityTimerRef.current);
      }
      if ((window as any).__isProcessingBackup || (window as any).__isPickingFile) {
        // Pause auto-lock completely during backup generation/restoration or file picking
        return;
      }
      inactivityTimerRef.current = window.setTimeout(() => {
        lockVault();
      }, mins * 60 * 1000);
    };

    resetTimer();

    const activityEvents = ['mousedown', 'mousemove', 'keydown', 'scroll', 'touchstart', 'pointerdown', 'click'];
    activityEvents.forEach((evt) => window.addEventListener(evt, resetTimer, { passive: true }));

    return () => {
      if (inactivityTimerRef.current) {
        window.clearTimeout(inactivityTimerRef.current);
        inactivityTimerRef.current = null;
      }
      activityEvents.forEach((evt) => window.removeEventListener(evt, resetTimer));
    };
  }, [vaultStatus, autoLockMinutes, lockVault]);

  // Robust File Picker protection: keeps auto-lock completely paused while Android File Picker is active
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleFilePickerClick = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      const target = e.target as HTMLElement;
      if (!target) return;

      const isFileInput = target.tagName === 'INPUT' && (target as HTMLInputElement).type === 'file';
      const isLabelForFile = target.closest('label') && target.closest('label')?.querySelector('input[type="file"]');

      if (isFileInput || isLabelForFile) {
        (window as any).__isPickingFile = true;
        (window as any).__filePickingStartTime = Date.now();
      }
    };

    // When the user finishes picking or cancels, reset the flag safely with a 4s grace window
    const handleFileChangeOrCancel = (e: Event) => {
      const target = e.target as HTMLElement;
      if (target && target.tagName === 'INPUT' && (target as HTMLInputElement).type === 'file') {
        setTimeout(() => {
          (window as any).__isPickingFile = false;
        }, 4000);
      }
    };

    window.addEventListener('click', handleFilePickerClick, true);
    window.addEventListener('change', handleFileChangeOrCancel, true);
    window.addEventListener('cancel', handleFileChangeOrCancel, true);

    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('click', handleFilePickerClick, true);
      window.removeEventListener('change', handleFileChangeOrCancel, true);
      window.removeEventListener('cancel', handleFileChangeOrCancel, true);
    };
  }, []);

  // Lock on tab visibility change, minimize or pagehide
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!lockOnTabSwitch) return;

    const isUserPickingFiles = () => {
      if ((window as any).__isPickingFile) return true;
      const startTime = (window as any).__filePickingStartTime || 0;
      if (startTime > 0 && Date.now() - startTime < 180000) return true;
      return false;
    };

    const handleVisibilityChange = () => {
      // Ignore if user is actively picking files or processing backup
      if (isUserPickingFiles() || (window as any).__isProcessingBackup) {
        return;
      }

      if (document.visibilityState === 'hidden' && vaultStatus === 'unlocked') {
        // Bloquear inmediatamente al minimizar o pasar a segundo plano
        lockVault();
      }
    };

    const handlePageHide = () => {
      if (isUserPickingFiles() || (window as any).__isProcessingBackup) {
        return;
      }
      if (vaultStatus === 'unlocked') {
        lockVault();
      }
    };

    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', handleVisibilityChange);
    window.addEventListener('pagehide', handlePageHide);
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', handleVisibilityChange);
      window.removeEventListener('pagehide', handlePageHide);
    };
  }, [lockOnTabSwitch, vaultStatus, lockVault]);

  // Panic key: Escape locks immediately
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape' && vaultStatus === 'unlocked' && !isSettingsOpen) {
        lockVault();
      }
    };

    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
  }, [vaultStatus, isSettingsOpen, lockVault]);

  // Reset Vault Handler
  const handleResetVault = async () => {
    try {
      await clearVaultDatabase();
      try {
        sessionStorage.removeItem('vault_session_pin');
      } catch {}
      localStorage.removeItem('vault_salt');
      localStorage.removeItem('vault_test_iv');
      localStorage.removeItem('vault_test_cipher');
      localStorage.removeItem('vault_initialized');
      localStorage.removeItem('vault_pin_length');

      setMasterKey(null);
      setIsSettingsOpen(false);
      setVaultStatus('setup');
      showToast('Bóveda restablecida por completo', 'alert');
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Error resetting vault:', err);
      showToast('Error al restablecer la bóveda', 'error');
    }
  };

  // Preference change listeners
  const handleAutoLockChange = (mins: number) => {
    setAutoLockMinutes(mins);
    localStorage.setItem('vault_autolock_mins', mins.toString());
  };

  const handleLockOnTabSwitchChange = (enabled: boolean) => {
    setLockOnTabSwitch(enabled);
    localStorage.setItem('vault_lock_on_tab_switch', enabled ? 'true' : 'false');
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#090d16] text-slate-100 flex flex-col font-sans select-none w-full max-w-full overflow-x-hidden">
      {/* Toast Notification Layer */}
      <ToastContainer toasts={toasts} />

      {/* Screen Routing */}
      {vaultStatus === 'checking' && (
        <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center">
          <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full border-2 border-indigo-500 border-t-transparent animate-spin" />
        </div>
      )}

      {vaultStatus === 'setup' && (
        <SetupVaultView
          onSetupComplete={(key, pin) => handleUnlock(key, pin)}
          showToast={showToast}
        />
      )}

      {vaultStatus === 'locked' && (
        <LockVaultView
          onUnlock={handleUnlock}
          onResetVaultRequest={handleResetVault}
          showToast={showToast}
        />
      )}

      {vaultStatus === 'unlocked' && masterKey && (
        <>
          <MainVaultView
            masterKey={masterKey}
            sessionPin={sessionPin}
            onLock={lockVault}
            onOpenSettings={() => setIsSettingsOpen(true)}
            showToast={showToast}
            refreshTrigger={vaultRefreshTrigger}
            autoLockMinutes={autoLockMinutes}
            onAutoLockChange={handleAutoLockChange}
            lockOnTabSwitch={lockOnTabSwitch}
            onLockOnTabSwitchChange={handleLockOnTabSwitchChange}
            onMasterKeyUpdated={(newKey) => {
              setMasterKey(newKey);
              setVaultRefreshTrigger((prev) => prev + 1);
            }}
            onResetVault={handleResetVault}
            hasUpdatePending={!!pendingUpdateInfo?.hasUpdate}
          />

          <SettingsModal
            isOpen={isSettingsOpen}
            onClose={() => setIsSettingsOpen(false)}
            masterKey={masterKey}
            sessionPin={sessionPin}
            onMasterKeyUpdated={(newKey) => {
              setMasterKey(newKey);
              setVaultRefreshTrigger((prev) => prev + 1);
            }}
            autoLockMinutes={autoLockMinutes}
            onAutoLockChange={handleAutoLockChange}
            lockOnTabSwitch={lockOnTabSwitch}
            onLockOnTabSwitchChange={handleLockOnTabSwitchChange}
            onPhotosUpdated={() => {
              setVaultRefreshTrigger((prev) => prev + 1);
            }}
            onResetVault={handleResetVault}
            showToast={showToast}
          />
        </>
      )}

      {/* Aviso flotante de Actualización Disponible al entrar */}
      {showUpdateBanner && pendingUpdateInfo && (
        <UpdateNotificationBanner
          updateInfo={pendingUpdateInfo}
          onOpenDetails={() => {
            setShowUpdateBanner(false);
            setShowUpdateModal(true);
          }}
          onApplyUpdate={() => updateService.applyUpdate()}
          onDismiss={() => {
            setShowUpdateBanner(false);
            if (pendingUpdateInfo.latestVersion) {
              updateService.dismissUpdate(pendingUpdateInfo.latestVersion);
            }
          }}
        />
      )}

      {/* Modal interactivo con Novedades de la Versión */}
      <UpdateNotificationModal
        isOpen={showUpdateModal}
        onClose={() => setShowUpdateModal(false)}
        updateInfo={pendingUpdateInfo}
        viewNotesOnly={false}
      />

      {/* Privacy Blur Shield when minimizing app or switching tasks */}
      {isPrivacyBlurActive && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[99999] backdrop-blur-3xl bg-[#060b14]/90 flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-6 text-center select-none animate-in fade-in duration-100 pointer-events-none">
          <div className="w-20 h-20 rounded-3xl bg-[#00e5ff]/10 border border-[#00e5ff]/30 flex items-center justify-center text-[#00e5ff] mb-4 shadow-[0_0_50px_rgba(0,229,255,0.25)] animate-pulse">
            <Shield className="w-10 h-10 stroke-[2]" />
          </div>
          <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-white tracking-tight mb-1">
            Mi Bóveda Protegida
          </h3>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 font-medium">
            Contenido privado oculto por seguridad
          </p>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
INICIO DE ARCHIVO: src/hooks/usePWAInstall.ts
================================================================================

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

interface BeforeInstallPromptEvent extends Event {
  prompt: () => Promise<void>;
  userChoice: Promise<{ outcome: 'accepted' | 'dismissed'; platform: string }>;
}

export function usePWAInstall() {
  const [deferredPrompt, setDeferredPrompt] = useState<BeforeInstallPromptEvent | null>(null);
  const [isInstalled, setIsInstalled] = useState(false);
  const [isIOS, setIsIOS] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    // Detect standalone mode (already installed on homescreen)
    const isStandalone =
      window.matchMedia('(display-mode: standalone)').matches ||
      (window.navigator as unknown as { standalone?: boolean }).standalone === true;
    setIsInstalled(isStandalone);

    // Detect iOS devices
    const userAgent = window.navigator.userAgent.toLowerCase();
    const isIOSDevice = /iphone|ipad|ipod/.test(userAgent);
    setIsIOS(isIOSDevice);

    const handleBeforeInstallPrompt = (e: Event) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      setDeferredPrompt(e as BeforeInstallPromptEvent);
    };

    const handleAppInstalled = () => {
      setIsInstalled(true);
      setDeferredPrompt(null);
    };

    window.addEventListener('beforeinstallprompt', handleBeforeInstallPrompt);
    window.addEventListener('appinstalled', handleAppInstalled);

    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('beforeinstallprompt', handleBeforeInstallPrompt);
      window.removeEventListener('appinstalled', handleAppInstalled);
    };
  }, []);

  const promptInstall = async (): Promise<boolean> => {
    if (!deferredPrompt) return false;
    await deferredPrompt.prompt();
    const choice = await deferredPrompt.userChoice;
    if (choice.outcome === 'accepted') {
      setIsInstalled(true);
      setDeferredPrompt(null);
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  };

  return {
    isInstallable: !!deferredPrompt,
    isInstalled,
    isIOS,
    promptInstall,
  };
}

================================================================================
INICIO DE ARCHIVO: src/services/backButtonService.ts
================================================================================

import { App as CapacitorApp } from '@capacitor/app';
import { Capacitor } from '@capacitor/core';

type BackHandler = () => boolean | void;

interface HandlerEntry {
  id: string;
  priority: number; // Higher number gets called first
  handler: BackHandler;
}

const handlers: HandlerEntry[] = [];
let isListenerAttached = false;
let lastBackPressTime = 0;

/**
 * Registers a back button action handler.
 * Handlers are executed in order of highest priority first.
 * If a handler returns true or undefined (non-false), back button propagation is stopped.
 * Returns an unregister cleanup function.
 */
export function registerBackHandler(id: string, handler: BackHandler, priority: number = 10): () => void {
  // Remove any existing handler with the same ID
  const index = handlers.findIndex((h) => h.id === id);
  if (index !== -1) {
    handlers.splice(index, 1);
  }

  handlers.push({ id, priority, handler });
  handlers.sort((a, b) => b.priority - a.priority);

  initBackButtonListener();

  return () => {
    const idx = handlers.findIndex((h) => h.id === id);
    if (idx !== -1) {
      handlers.splice(idx, 1);
    }
  };
}

/**
 * Initializes the back button event listeners for Android Capacitor and browser history
 */
export function initBackButtonListener() {
  if (isListenerAttached || typeof window === 'undefined') return;
  isListenerAttached = true;

  if (Capacitor.isNativePlatform()) {
    CapacitorApp.addListener('backButton', async (data) => {
      // Execute highest priority handler first
      for (const entry of [...handlers]) {
        try {
          const result = entry.handler();
          if (result !== false) {
            // Back action handled by component/modal/navigation level
            return;
          }
        } catch (e) {
          console.error('Error in back handler:', entry.id, e);
        }
      }

      // If at root of the app and no handler consumed the back press:
      const now = Date.now();
      if (now - lastBackPressTime < 2200) {
        // Double press back within 2.2s -> minimize or exit app
        try {
          await CapacitorApp.minimizeApp();
        } catch {
          await CapacitorApp.exitApp();
        }
      } else {
        lastBackPressTime = now;
        window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('vault_root_back_press', { detail: { time: now } }));
      }
    });
  }

  // Also handle popstate for web / PWA
  window.addEventListener('popstate', () => {
    for (const entry of [...handlers]) {
      try {
        const result = entry.handler();
        if (result !== false) {
          return;
        }
      } catch (e) {
        console.error('Error in popstate handler:', entry.id, e);
      }
    }

    // If at root of the app and no handler consumed the back press:
    const now = Date.now();
    if (now - lastBackPressTime < 2200) {
      window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('vault_root_exit_allowed', { detail: { time: now } }));
    } else {
      lastBackPressTime = now;
      try {
        window.history.pushState({ vaultRoot: true }, '');
      } catch {}
      window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('vault_root_back_press', { detail: { time: now } }));
    }
  });
}

/**
 * Triggers the back action programmatically (for screen swipe gestures, on-screen back buttons, etc.)
 * Executes the highest priority registered handler. Returns true if handled, false if at root.
 */
export function triggerBackAction(): boolean {
  for (const entry of [...handlers]) {
    try {
      const result = entry.handler();
      if (result !== false) {
        return true;
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('Error in triggerBackAction handler:', entry.id, e);
    }
  }

  // If at root of the app and no handler consumed the back press:
  const now = Date.now();
  if (now - lastBackPressTime < 2200) {
    window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('vault_root_exit_allowed', { detail: { time: now } }));
  } else {
    lastBackPressTime = now;
    window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('vault_root_back_press', { detail: { time: now } }));
  }
  return false;
}

================================================================================
INICIO DE ARCHIVO: src/services/biometricService.ts

/**
 * Biometric authentication service for Android (Native BiometricPrompt / Capacitor & WebAuthn / Passkeys).
 * Integrates directly with the device's native fingerprint scanner.
 */

import { registerPlugin, Capacitor } from '@capacitor/core';
import { bufToHex, hexToBuf, generateIV } from '../crypto/vaultCrypto';

const BIO_ENABLED_KEY = 'vault_biometric_enabled';
const BIO_CIPHER_KEY = 'vault_biometric_cipher';
const BIO_IV_KEY = 'vault_biometric_iv';
const BIO_WRAP_KEY = 'vault_biometric_wrap';
const BIO_CRED_ID_KEY = 'vault_biometric_cred_id';
const BIO_TYPE_KEY = 'vault_biometric_type'; // 'native' | 'webauthn'

export interface NativeVaultBiometricsPlugin {
  isAvailable(): Promise<{ isAvailable: boolean; hasEnrolled: boolean; status: string }>;
  authenticate(options?: {
    title?: string;
    subtitle?: string;
    negativeButton?: string;
  }): Promise<{ success: boolean; errorCode?: number; message?: string }>;
}

export const NativeVaultBiometrics = registerPlugin<NativeVaultBiometricsPlugin>('VaultBiometrics');

/**
 * Checks whether native Android / iOS biometrics plugin is accessible
 */
export async function isNativeBiometricPluginAvailable(): Promise<boolean> {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return false;

  // Check Capacitor native platform
  if (Capacitor.isNativePlatform()) {
    try {
      const res = await NativeVaultBiometrics.isAvailable();
      return !!res.isAvailable;
    } catch {
      // Fall through to window bridge
    }
  }

  // Check Android JavascriptInterface bridge (if custom WebView)
  if ((window as any).AndroidBiometrics?.isAvailable) {
    try {
      return !!(window as any).AndroidBiometrics.isAvailable();
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  }

  return false;
}

/**
 * Checks if biometric hardware (fingerprint sensor / platform authenticator) is available on the device.
 */
export async function isBiometricsSupported(): Promise<boolean> {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return false;

  // 1. Native Plugin / Bridge
  if (await isNativeBiometricPluginAvailable()) {
    return true;
  }

  // 2. WebAuthn / Platform Authenticator (Android Chrome / Google Play Services)
  if (window.PublicKeyCredential) {
    try {
      const available = await PublicKeyCredential.isUserVerifyingPlatformAuthenticatorAvailable();
      return !!available;
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  }

  return false;
}

/**
 * Checks if biometric credentials exist on this device.
 */
export function hasBiometricCredentials(): boolean {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return false;
  return (
    localStorage.getItem(BIO_ENABLED_KEY) === 'true' &&
    !!localStorage.getItem(BIO_CRED_ID_KEY) &&
    !!localStorage.getItem(BIO_CIPHER_KEY) &&
    !!localStorage.getItem(BIO_IV_KEY) &&
    !!localStorage.getItem(BIO_WRAP_KEY)
  );
}

/**
 * Checks if biometric fingerprint unlock is already enrolled and active.
 */
export function isBiometricsEnrolled(): boolean {
  return hasBiometricCredentials();
}

/**
 * Checks if automatic prompt on startup is enabled.
 */
export function isBiometricsAutoPromptEnabled(): boolean {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return false;
  return hasBiometricCredentials() && localStorage.getItem('vault_biometrics_auto_prompt') !== 'false';
}

/**
 * Toggles whether biometrics prompts automatically on lock screen opening.
 */
export function setBiometricsAutoPromptEnabled(enabled: boolean): void {
  localStorage.setItem('vault_biometrics_auto_prompt', enabled ? 'true' : 'false');
  localStorage.setItem('vault_biometrics_active', enabled ? 'true' : 'false');
}

/**
 * Gets clean relying party ID from current origin hostname
 */
function getRelyingPartyId(): string {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return 'localhost';
  const hostname = window.location.hostname.split(':')[0];
  if (!hostname || hostname === '' || hostname === '127.0.0.1') return 'localhost';
  return hostname;
}

/**
 * Enrolls the device's biometric fingerprint and encrypts the vault PIN with a dedicated wrapping key.
 */
export async function enrollBiometrics(pin: string): Promise<boolean> {
  const isNative = await isNativeBiometricPluginAvailable();

  // 1. If Native Android Biometric plugin is present
  if (isNative) {
    try {
      let authSuccess = false;
      if (Capacitor.isNativePlatform()) {
        const auth = await NativeVaultBiometrics.authenticate({
          title: 'Vincular Huella Digital',
          subtitle: 'Toca el sensor de huella para confirmar',
          negativeButton: 'Cancelar',
        });
        authSuccess = !!auth.success;
      } else if ((window as any).AndroidBiometrics?.authenticate) {
        authSuccess = !!(window as any).AndroidBiometrics.authenticate('Vincular Huella Digital');
      }

      if (!authSuccess) {
        throw new Error('No se pudo verificar la huella en el sensor.');
      }

      // Generate local encryption wrap key for PIN
      const rawWrapKey = window.crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(32));
      const cryptoWrapKey = await window.crypto.subtle.importKey(
        'raw',
        rawWrapKey,
        { name: 'AES-GCM' },
        false,
        ['encrypt', 'decrypt']
      );

      const iv = generateIV();
      const encodedPin = new TextEncoder().encode(pin);
      const encryptedPin = await window.crypto.subtle.encrypt(
        { name: 'AES-GCM', iv: iv as BufferSource },
        cryptoWrapKey,
        encodedPin
      );

      localStorage.setItem(BIO_CRED_ID_KEY, 'native_device_biometric');
      localStorage.setItem(BIO_WRAP_KEY, bufToHex(rawWrapKey));
      localStorage.setItem(BIO_IV_KEY, bufToHex(iv));
      localStorage.setItem(BIO_CIPHER_KEY, bufToHex(new Uint8Array(encryptedPin)));
      localStorage.setItem(BIO_TYPE_KEY, 'native');
      localStorage.setItem(BIO_ENABLED_KEY, 'true');
      return true;
    } catch (nativeErr: any) {
      console.error('Native biometric enrollment error:', nativeErr);
      throw new Error(nativeErr?.message || 'Error al autenticar con el sensor nativo de huella.');
    }
  }

  // 2. WebAuthn fallback (Chrome, Firefox, Safari, Edge)
  if (!window.PublicKeyCredential) {
    // Check if inside a WebView (like WebIntoApp or Android WebView)
    const ua = navigator.userAgent || '';
    const isWebView = /wv|Android.*Version\/[0-9.]+/i.test(ua);
    if (isWebView) {
      throw new Error(
        'El visor WebView de esta app (WebIntoApp) tiene bloqueado el acceso al sensor de huellas. Para usar tu huella: 1) Abre la app en Chrome en tu celular e instálala con "Añadir a pantalla de inicio", o 2) Desactiva el Modo Avión.'
      );
    }
    throw new Error(
      'Tu navegador o visor actual no tiene habilitada la API de huella biométrica (WebAuthn). Desactiva el Modo Avión o abre la app en Chrome.'
    );
  }

  const rpId = getRelyingPartyId();
  const challenge = window.crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(32));
  const userId = window.crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(16));

  let credential: PublicKeyCredential | null = null;
  try {
    credential = (await navigator.credentials.create({
      publicKey: {
        challenge,
        rp: {
          name: 'Mi Bóveda',
          id: rpId,
        },
        user: {
          id: userId,
          name: 'boveda_user',
          displayName: 'Usuario Bóveda',
        },
        pubKeyCredParams: [
          { alg: -7, type: 'public-key' }, // ES256
          { alg: -257, type: 'public-key' }, // RS256
        ],
        authenticatorSelection: {
          authenticatorAttachment: 'platform', // Native fingerprint scanner
          userVerification: 'required',
          residentKey: 'discouraged',
          requireResidentKey: false,
        },
        timeout: 60000,
        attestation: 'none',
      },
    })) as PublicKeyCredential | null;
  } catch (err: any) {
    console.error('Error creating credential:', err);
    if (err?.name === 'NotAllowedError') {
      throw new Error('Registro cancelado en el sensor de huella.');
    }
    if (
      err?.message?.toLowerCase().includes('credential manager') ||
      err?.name === 'UnknownError' ||
      err?.message?.toLowerCase().includes('offline')
    ) {
      throw new Error(
        'El Administrador de Credenciales de Google falló. Si tienes el "Modo avión" activado en tu celular, desactívalo para permitir la vinculación de huella.'
      );
    }
    throw new Error(
      err?.message || 'No se pudo registrar la huella. Desactiva el Modo Avión y prueba de nuevo.'
    );
  }

  if (!credential || !credential.rawId) {
    throw new Error('No se pudo verificar la huella digital.');
  }

  // 3. Generate random 256-bit wrapping key for encrypting the PIN locally
  const rawWrapKey = window.crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(32));
  const cryptoWrapKey = await window.crypto.subtle.importKey(
    'raw',
    rawWrapKey,
    { name: 'AES-GCM' },
    false,
    ['encrypt', 'decrypt']
  );

  const iv = generateIV();
  const encodedPin = new TextEncoder().encode(pin);
  const encryptedPin = await window.crypto.subtle.encrypt(
    { name: 'AES-GCM', iv: iv as BufferSource },
    cryptoWrapKey,
    encodedPin
  );

  // 4. Store credential ID, encrypted PIN and wrap key
  const credRawIdHex = bufToHex(new Uint8Array(credential.rawId));
  localStorage.setItem(BIO_CRED_ID_KEY, credRawIdHex);
  localStorage.setItem(BIO_WRAP_KEY, bufToHex(rawWrapKey));
  localStorage.setItem(BIO_IV_KEY, bufToHex(iv));
  localStorage.setItem(BIO_CIPHER_KEY, bufToHex(new Uint8Array(encryptedPin)));
  localStorage.setItem(BIO_TYPE_KEY, 'webauthn');
  localStorage.setItem(BIO_ENABLED_KEY, 'true');

  return true;
}

// Mutex to strictly prevent concurrent or duplicated biometric authentication calls
let activeBiometricAuthPromise: Promise<string> | null = null;
let currentBioAbortController: AbortController | null = null;

/**
 * Triggers the native Android fingerprint prompt and, upon successful touch, returns the decrypted PIN.
 * Uses a singleton promise to ensure only one biometric prompt is active at a time.
 * If forceFresh is true, cancels any stalled prompt and starts a fresh biometric scan.
 */
export async function authenticateWithBiometrics(forceFresh = false): Promise<string> {
  if (forceFresh) {
    if (currentBioAbortController) {
      try {
        currentBioAbortController.abort();
      } catch {}
      currentBioAbortController = null;
    }
    activeBiometricAuthPromise = null;
  }

  if (activeBiometricAuthPromise) {
    return activeBiometricAuthPromise;
  }

  const abortCtrl = new AbortController();
  currentBioAbortController = abortCtrl;

  activeBiometricAuthPromise = (async () => {
    try {
      if (!isBiometricsEnrolled()) {
        throw new Error('La huella digital aún no está registrada en esta bóveda.');
      }

      const credIdHex = localStorage.getItem(BIO_CRED_ID_KEY);
      const rawWrapHex = localStorage.getItem(BIO_WRAP_KEY);
      const ivHex = localStorage.getItem(BIO_IV_KEY);
      const cipherHex = localStorage.getItem(BIO_CIPHER_KEY);
      const bioType = localStorage.getItem(BIO_TYPE_KEY) || 'webauthn';

      if (!credIdHex || !rawWrapHex || !ivHex || !cipherHex) {
        disableBiometrics();
        throw new Error('Datos de huella corruptos. Por favor vincula tu huella nuevamente.');
      }

      // 1. If enrolled via Native Plugin / Bridge
      if (bioType === 'native' || credIdHex === 'native_device_biometric') {
        let verified = false;
        if (Capacitor.isNativePlatform()) {
          const auth = await NativeVaultBiometrics.authenticate({
            title: 'Desbloquear Bóveda',
            subtitle: 'Toca el sensor de huella para ingresar',
            negativeButton: 'Usar PIN',
          });
          verified = !!auth.success;
        } else if ((window as any).AndroidBiometrics?.authenticate) {
          verified = !!(window as any).AndroidBiometrics.authenticate('Desbloquear Bóveda');
        }

        if (!verified) {
          throw new Error('Huella no reconocida o autenticación cancelada.');
        }
      } else {
        // 2. WebAuthn Authentication Dialog
        const credRawId = hexToBuf(credIdHex);
        const rpId = getRelyingPartyId();
        const challenge = window.crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(32));

        let assertion: Credential | null = null;
        try {
          assertion = await navigator.credentials.get({
            publicKey: {
              challenge,
              rpId,
              allowCredentials: [
                {
                  type: 'public-key',
                  id: credRawId as BufferSource,
                  transports: ['internal'],
                },
              ],
              userVerification: 'required',
              timeout: 60000,
            },
            signal: abortCtrl.signal,
          });
        } catch (err: any) {
          console.error('Error getting credential:', err);
          if (err?.name === 'AbortError') {
            throw new Error('Solicitud biométrica cancelada para iniciar una nueva.');
          }
          if (err?.name === 'NotAllowedError') {
            throw err;
          }
          if (err?.name === 'InvalidStateError' || err?.name === 'NotFoundError') {
            disableBiometrics();
            throw new Error('El registro de huella expiró. Toca el sensor para vincularla de nuevo con tu PIN.');
          }
          if (
            err?.message?.toLowerCase().includes('credential manager') ||
            err?.name === 'UnknownError'
          ) {
            throw new Error(
              'Google Credential Manager falló. Si tienes el "Modo avión" activado en tu celular, desactívalo un momento para verificar tu huella, o ingresa con tu PIN.'
            );
          }
          throw err;
        }

        if (!assertion) {
          throw new Error('No se reconoció la huella digital.');
        }
      }

      // 3. Biometrics passed! Decrypt the PIN
      const rawWrapKey = hexToBuf(rawWrapHex);
      const iv = hexToBuf(ivHex);
      const cipher = hexToBuf(cipherHex);

      const cryptoWrapKey = await window.crypto.subtle.importKey(
        'raw',
        rawWrapKey as BufferSource,
        { name: 'AES-GCM' },
        false,
        ['decrypt']
      );

      const decryptedBytes = await window.crypto.subtle.decrypt(
        { name: 'AES-GCM', iv: iv as BufferSource },
        cryptoWrapKey,
        cipher as BufferSource
      );

      return new TextDecoder().decode(decryptedBytes);
    } finally {
      // Clear mutex and controller on finish
      if (currentBioAbortController === abortCtrl) {
        currentBioAbortController = null;
      }
      activeBiometricAuthPromise = null;
    }
  })();

  return activeBiometricAuthPromise;
}

/**
 * Disables and removes biometric credentials from storage.
 */
export function disableBiometrics(): void {
  localStorage.removeItem(BIO_ENABLED_KEY);
  localStorage.removeItem(BIO_CRED_ID_KEY);
  localStorage.removeItem(BIO_CIPHER_KEY);
  localStorage.removeItem(BIO_IV_KEY);
  localStorage.removeItem(BIO_WRAP_KEY);
  localStorage.removeItem(BIO_TYPE_KEY);
}

import { Capacitor, registerPlugin } from '@capacitor/core';

export interface NativeMediaItemIdentifier {
  filename: string;
  size?: number;
  lastModified?: number;
  mimeType?: string;
  uri?: string;
}

export interface NativeVaultMediaPlugin {
  /**
   * Elimina un archivo multimedia de la galería pública usando MediaStore en Android
   */
  deleteFromGallery(options: {
    uri?: string;
    filename?: string;
    size?: number;
    lastModified?: number;
  }): Promise<{ success: boolean; message?: string }>;

  /**
   * Elimina por lotes un conjunto de archivos multimedia de la galería pública usando un único
   * diálogo del sistema en Android 11+ (MediaStore.createDeleteRequest).
   */
  deleteBatchFromGallery(options: {
    items: NativeMediaItemIdentifier[];
  }): Promise<{
    success: boolean;
    requested?: boolean;
    confirmedByUser?: boolean;
    matchedCount?: number;
    deletedCount?: number;
    message?: string;
    error?: string;
  }>;

  /**
   * Abre la aplicación nativa de Galería del dispositivo
   */
  openGalleryApp(): Promise<{ success: boolean; error?: string }>;

  /**
   * Guarda y restaura un archivo multimedia o documento en el almacenamiento público del dispositivo / galería (MediaStore)
   */
  exportMediaToDevice(options: {
    base64Data: string;
    filename: string;
    mimeType?: string;
    category?: string;
  }): Promise<{ success: boolean; uri?: string; path?: string; message?: string }>;
}

// Registro del plugin nativo de Capacitor (se enlaza en Android con VaultMediaPlugin.java)
export const NativeVaultMedia = registerPlugin<NativeVaultMediaPlugin>('VaultMedia');

/**
 * Retorna true si la app se está ejecutando como un APK nativo en Android o iOS
 */
export const isNativeApp = (): boolean => {
  return Capacitor.isNativePlatform();
};

/**
 * Abre la app nativa de galería de fotos de Android o iOS de manera directa
 */
export const openNativeGallery = async (): Promise<boolean> => {
  if (isNativeApp()) {
    try {
      const res = await NativeVaultMedia.openGalleryApp();
      if (res && res.success) return true;
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn('Could not launch gallery via native plugin:', e);
    }
  }

  try {
    const isAndroid = /Android/i.test(navigator.userAgent);
    if (isAndroid) {
      // Intent standard de Android para ver la Galería multimedia
      window.location.href = 'intent:#Intent;action=android.intent.action.VIEW;type=image/*;end';
    } else {
      window.open('photos-redirect://', '_blank');
    }
  } catch {}
  return true;
};

/**
 * Abre el explorador de archivos / gestor de descargas en Android
 */
export const openNativeFileManager = async (): Promise<boolean> => {
  try {
    const isAndroid = /Android/i.test(navigator.userAgent);
    if (isAndroid) {
      // Abre el gestor de archivos / descargas de Android
      window.location.href = 'intent:#Intent;action=android.intent.action.GET_CONTENT;type=*/*;category=android.intent.category.OPENABLE;end';
    } else {
      window.open('shareddocuments://', '_blank');
    }
  } catch {}
  return true;
};

/**
 * Función unificada para borrar un archivo individual de la galería pública.
 */
export const requestNativeGalleryDeletion = async (
  filename: string,
  uri?: string,
  size?: number,
  lastModified?: number
): Promise<{ handledNatively: boolean; success: boolean }> => {
  if (!isNativeApp()) {
    return { handledNatively: false, success: false };
  }

  try {
    (window as any).__isPickingFile = true;
    (window as any).__filePickingStartTime = Date.now();
    const result = await NativeVaultMedia.deleteFromGallery({ uri, filename, size, lastModified });
    return { handledNatively: true, success: result.success };
  } catch (error) {
    console.warn('Native deletion not handled or rejected by user:', error);
    return { handledNatively: true, success: false };
  }
};

/**
 * Función por lotes para borrar múltiples archivos originales con un solo diálogo nativo.
 */
export const requestNativeBatchGalleryDeletion = async (
  items: NativeMediaItemIdentifier[]
): Promise<{ handledNatively: boolean; success: boolean; requested?: boolean }> => {
  if (!isNativeApp() || !items || items.length === 0) {
    return { handledNatively: false, success: false };
  }

  // 1. Intento por lote único
  try {
    (window as any).__isPickingFile = true;
    (window as any).__filePickingStartTime = Date.now();
    const result = await NativeVaultMedia.deleteBatchFromGallery({ items });
    if (result && (result.requested || result.success)) {
      return {
        handledNatively: true,
        success: result.success,
        requested: true,
      };
    }
  } catch (error) {
    console.warn('Native batch deletion error, trying single fallback:', error);
  }

  // 2. Fallback de respaldo: si el borrado por lote no fue ejecutado por el sistema,
  // intentar solicitar eliminación individual para cada archivo (el método que sí funciona en Android)
  try {
    let anyHandled = false;
    for (const item of items) {
      const res = await NativeVaultMedia.deleteFromGallery({
        filename: item.filename,
        uri: item.uri,
        size: item.size,
        lastModified: item.lastModified,
      });
      if (res && res.success) {
        anyHandled = true;
      }
    }
    if (anyHandled) {
      return { handledNatively: true, success: true, requested: true };
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('Fallback individual deletion error:', err);
  }

  return { handledNatively: false, success: false };
};

/**
 * Convierte un Blob a cadena base64 limpia (sin prefijo data URI)
 */
export const blobToBase64 = (blob: Blob): Promise<string> => {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onloadend = () => {
      const dataUrl = (reader.result as string) || '';
      const base64 = dataUrl.includes(',') ? dataUrl.split(',')[1] : dataUrl;
      resolve(base64);
    };
    reader.onerror = reject;
    reader.readAsDataURL(blob);
  });
};

/**
 * Guarda y restaura un archivo de la bóveda en la galería nativa de Android o almacenamiento del teléfono.
 * En la web, descarga el archivo directamente.
 */
export const exportVaultItemToDevice = async (item: {
  blobUrl: string;
  filename: string;
  mimeType?: string;
  category?: string;
}): Promise<{ success: boolean; error?: string }> => {
  try {
    const res = await fetch(item.blobUrl);
    const blob = await res.blob();
    const effectiveMime = item.mimeType || blob.type || 'application/octet-stream';

    if (isNativeApp()) {
      try {
        const base64Data = await blobToBase64(blob);
        const nativeRes = await NativeVaultMedia.exportMediaToDevice({
          base64Data,
          filename: item.filename,
          mimeType: effectiveMime,
          category: item.category || 'photo',
        });
        if (nativeRes && nativeRes.success) {
          return { success: true };
        }
      } catch (nativeErr: any) {
        console.warn('Native export failed, attempting web download fallback:', nativeErr);
      }
    }

    // Web / Fallback standard download
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const link = document.createElement('a');
    link.href = url;
    link.download = item.filename;
    document.body.appendChild(link);
    link.click();
    document.body.removeChild(link);
    setTimeout(() => {
      try {
        URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
      } catch {}
    }, 60000);

    return { success: true };
  } catch (err: any) {
    console.error('Error in exportVaultItemToDevice:', err);
    return { success: false, error: err?.message || 'Error al exportar archivo' };
  }
};

================================================================================
INICIO DE ARCHIVO: src/services/recoveryKeyService.ts

import {
  bufToHex,
  hexToBuf,
  generateSalt,
  generateIV,
  deriveKeyFromPin,
  createVerificationPayload,
  exportMasterKeyRaw,
  importMasterKeyRaw,
  deriveRecoveryWrapKey,
  encryptBuffer,
  decryptBuffer,
} from '../crypto/vaultCrypto';
import { getAllEncryptedItems, saveEncryptedItems } from '../db/vaultDb';

const STORAGE_WRAPPED_KEY = 'vault_recovery_key_wrapped_data';
const STORAGE_WRAPPED_IV = 'vault_recovery_key_wrapped_iv';
const STORAGE_SALT = 'vault_recovery_key_salt';
const STORAGE_VERIFY_IV = 'vault_recovery_key_verify_iv';
const STORAGE_VERIFY_CIPHER = 'vault_recovery_key_verify_cipher';
const STORAGE_CONFIGURED = 'vault_recovery_key_configured';
const STORAGE_CREATED_AT = 'vault_recovery_key_created_at';

const RECOVERY_CHARSET = 'ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ23456789'; // 32 chars, excludes 0, O, 1, I
const RECOVERY_KEY_LENGTH = 16;
const VERIFICATION_TEST_PHRASE = 'VAULT_MASTER_RECOVERY_KEY_VERIFIED_V1';

/**
 * Generates an unpredictable 16-character recovery key formatted as XXXX-XXXX-XXXX-XXXX
 */
export function generateRecoveryKey(): string {
  const randomBytes = new Uint8Array(RECOVERY_KEY_LENGTH);
  window.crypto.getRandomValues(randomBytes);

  let raw = '';
  for (let i = 0; i < RECOVERY_KEY_LENGTH; i++) {
    const index = randomBytes[i] % RECOVERY_CHARSET.length;
    raw += RECOVERY_CHARSET[index];
  }

  return formatRecoveryKeyDisplay(raw);
}

/**
 * Normalizes a recovery key by removing spaces, dashes, and converting to uppercase
 */
export function normalizeRecoveryKey(raw: string): string {
  return raw.toUpperCase().replace(/[^A-Z0-9]/g, '');
}

/**
 * Formats a raw or partial string into groups of 4 separated by hyphens (e.g. ABCD-EFGH-JKLM-NPQR)
 */
export function formatRecoveryKeyDisplay(raw: string): string {
  const clean = normalizeRecoveryKey(raw);
  const parts: string[] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < clean.length && i < 16; i += 4) {
    parts.push(clean.substring(i, i + 4));
  }
  return parts.join('-');
}

/**
 * Checks if a master recovery key is configured
 */
export function isRecoveryKeyConfigured(): boolean {
  try {
    return (
      localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_CONFIGURED) === 'true' &&
      !!localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_WRAPPED_KEY) &&
      !!localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_SALT)
    );
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

/**
 * Disables and clears the configured recovery key
 */
export function disableRecoveryKey(): void {
  try {
    localStorage.removeItem(STORAGE_CONFIGURED);
    localStorage.removeItem(STORAGE_WRAPPED_KEY);
    localStorage.removeItem(STORAGE_WRAPPED_IV);
    localStorage.removeItem(STORAGE_SALT);
    localStorage.removeItem(STORAGE_VERIFY_IV);
    localStorage.removeItem(STORAGE_VERIFY_CIPHER);
    localStorage.removeItem(STORAGE_CREATED_AT);
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('Error disabling recovery key:', err);
  }
}

/**
 * Gets timestamp when recovery key was created
 */
export function getRecoveryKeyCreatedAt(): number | null {
  try {
    const val = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_CREATED_AT);
    return val ? Number(val) : null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

/**
 * Encrypts and backs up the current master key with a recovery key escrow wrapper
 */
export async function backupMasterKeyWithRecoveryKey(
  masterKey: CryptoKey,
  recoveryKey: string
): Promise<void> {
  const normalized = normalizeRecoveryKey(recoveryKey);
  if (normalized.length !== 16) {
    throw new Error('La clave de recuperación debe tener 16 caracteres.');
  }

  const rawMaster = await exportMasterKeyRaw(masterKey);
  const salt = generateSalt();
  const secretPhrase = `VAULT_ESCROW::${normalized}::AES256_V1`;

  const wrapKey = await deriveRecoveryWrapKey(secretPhrase, salt);
  const encrypted = await encryptBuffer(rawMaster, wrapKey);

  // Verification test payload to validate entered keys without corrupting anything
  const verifyIv = generateIV();
  const verifyEnc = await window.crypto.subtle.encrypt(
    { name: 'AES-GCM', iv: verifyIv as BufferSource },
    wrapKey,
    new TextEncoder().encode(VERIFICATION_TEST_PHRASE)
  );

  localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_CONFIGURED, 'true');
  localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_SALT, bufToHex(salt));
  localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_WRAPPED_IV, bufToHex(new Uint8Array(encrypted.iv)));
  localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_WRAPPED_KEY, bufToHex(new Uint8Array(encrypted.data)));
  localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_VERIFY_IV, bufToHex(verifyIv));
  localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_VERIFY_CIPHER, bufToHex(new Uint8Array(verifyEnc)));
  localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_CREATED_AT, Date.now().toString());
}

/**
 * Verifies if the entered recovery key matches the stored escrow configuration
 */
export async function verifyRecoveryKey(
  recoveryKey: string
): Promise<{ valid: boolean; error?: string }> {
  try {
    const normalized = normalizeRecoveryKey(recoveryKey);
    if (normalized.length !== 16) {
      return {
        valid: false,
        error: 'La clave de recuperación debe tener exactamente 16 caracteres (4 bloques de 4).',
      };
    }

    const saltHex = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_SALT);
    const verifyIvHex = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_VERIFY_IV);
    const verifyCipherHex = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_VERIFY_CIPHER);

    if (!saltHex || !verifyIvHex || !verifyCipherHex) {
      return {
        valid: false,
        error: 'No se encontró una clave de recuperación configurada en esta bóveda.',
      };
    }

    const salt = hexToBuf(saltHex);
    const verifyIv = hexToBuf(verifyIvHex);
    const verifyCipher = hexToBuf(verifyCipherHex);

    const secretPhrase = `VAULT_ESCROW::${normalized}::AES256_V1`;
    const wrapKey = await deriveRecoveryWrapKey(secretPhrase, salt);

    const decrypted = await window.crypto.subtle.decrypt(
      { name: 'AES-GCM', iv: verifyIv as BufferSource },
      wrapKey,
      verifyCipher as BufferSource
    );

    const text = new TextDecoder().decode(decrypted);
    if (text === VERIFICATION_TEST_PHRASE) {
      return { valid: true };
    }

    return {
      valid: false,
      error: 'La clave de recuperación no es válida. Revisa los caracteres e intenta de nuevo.',
    };
  } catch {
    return {
      valid: false,
      error: 'Clave de recuperación incorrecta. Verifica las letras y números.',
    };
  }
}

/**
 * Unwraps the original master key using the verified recovery key
 */
export async function unwrapMasterKeyWithRecoveryKey(
  recoveryKey: string
): Promise<CryptoKey | null> {
  try {
    const normalized = normalizeRecoveryKey(recoveryKey);
    const saltHex = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_SALT);
    const ivHex = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_WRAPPED_IV);
    const wrappedHex = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_WRAPPED_KEY);

    if (!saltHex || !ivHex || !wrappedHex) {
      return null;
    }

    const salt = hexToBuf(saltHex);
    const iv = Array.from(hexToBuf(ivHex));
    const encryptedBuf = hexToBuf(wrappedHex);
    const encryptedData = encryptedBuf.buffer.slice(
      encryptedBuf.byteOffset,
      encryptedBuf.byteOffset + encryptedBuf.byteLength
    ) as ArrayBuffer;

    const secretPhrase = `VAULT_ESCROW::${normalized}::AES256_V1`;
    const wrapKey = await deriveRecoveryWrapKey(secretPhrase, salt);

    const rawMaster = await decryptBuffer(encryptedData, iv, wrapKey);
    return await importMasterKeyRaw(rawMaster);
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('Failed to unwrap recovery master key:', err);
    return null;
  }
}

/**
 * Resets the master PIN using the recovery key:
 * Re-encrypts all existing items to the new PIN key and updates verification tokens.
 * Zero photos or videos are lost!
 */
export async function executePinResetWithRecoveryKey(
  recoveryKey: string,
  newPin: string
): Promise<{ success: boolean; masterKey: CryptoKey; error?: string }> {
  try {
    const verification = await verifyRecoveryKey(recoveryKey);
    if (!verification.valid) {
      return {
        success: false,
        masterKey: null as any,
        error: verification.error || 'Clave de recuperación incorrecta',
      };
    }

    // 1. Unwrap the original master key
    const originalMasterKey = await unwrapMasterKeyWithRecoveryKey(recoveryKey);

    // 2. Generate new salt and derive new key for the new PIN
    const newSalt = generateSalt();
    const newMasterKey = await deriveKeyFromPin(newPin, newSalt);

    // 3. Re-encrypt all items in IndexedDB from original master key to new master key
    if (originalMasterKey) {
      const allItems = await getAllEncryptedItems();
      const updatedItems = [];

      for (const item of allItems) {
        try {
          const decrypted = await decryptBuffer(item.data, item.iv, originalMasterKey);
          const reEncrypted = await encryptBuffer(decrypted, newMasterKey);
          updatedItems.push({
            ...item,
            iv: reEncrypted.iv,
            data: reEncrypted.data,
          });
        } catch (itemErr) {
          console.warn(`Could not re-encrypt item ${item.id}:`, itemErr);
          updatedItems.push(item);
        }
      }

      if (updatedItems.length > 0) {
        await saveEncryptedItems(updatedItems);
      }
    }

    // 4. Create new verification payload
    const newVerification = await createVerificationPayload(newMasterKey);

    // 5. Update localStorage credentials
    localStorage.setItem('vault_salt', bufToHex(newSalt));
    localStorage.setItem('vault_test_iv', newVerification.ivHex);
    localStorage.setItem('vault_test_cipher', newVerification.cipherHex);
    localStorage.setItem('vault_pin_length', newPin.length.toString());

    try {
      sessionStorage.setItem('vault_session_pin', newPin);
    } catch {}

    // 6. Keep the recovery escrow active and wrapped for the new master key
    await backupMasterKeyWithRecoveryKey(newMasterKey, recoveryKey);

    return {
      success: true,
      masterKey: newMasterKey,
    };
  } catch (err: any) {
    console.error('Error executing PIN reset with recovery key:', err);
    return {
      success: false,
      masterKey: null as any,
      error: err?.message || 'Error al restablecer el PIN de la bóveda',
    };
  }
}

/**
 * Downloads a formatted .txt backup file containing the recovery key and instructions
 */
export function downloadRecoveryKeyFile(recoveryKey: string): void {
  const formatted = formatRecoveryKeyDisplay(recoveryKey);
  const dateStr = new Date().toLocaleDateString('es-ES', {
    year: 'numeric',
    month: 'long',
    day: 'numeric',
    hour: '2-digit',
    minute: '2-digit',
  });

  const content = `=====================================================
BÓVEDA ULTRA SEGURA - CLAVE DE RECUPERACIÓN MAESTRA
=====================================================

TU CLAVE DE RECUPERACIÓN:
-----------------------------------------------------
${formatted}
-----------------------------------------------------

Fecha de registro: ${dateStr}

INSTRUCCIONES IMPORTANTES:
1. Guarda este archivo en un lugar seguro (memoria USB, notas protegidas o impreso en papel).
2. Si alguna vez olvidas tu PIN para ingresar a la bóveda, ve a la pantalla de bloqueo y presiona "¿Olvidaste tu PIN?".
3. Ingresa esta clave de 16 caracteres para crear un nuevo PIN.
4. Gracias a esta clave, NUNCA perderás tus fotos ni tus videos protegidos aunque olvides el PIN.
5. No compartas esta clave con nadie.

=====================================================
`;

  const blob = new Blob([content], { type: 'text/plain;charset=utf-8' });
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a = document.createElement('a');
  a.href = url;
  a.download = `Clave_Recuperacion_Boveda_${formatted}.txt`;
  document.body.appendChild(a);
  a.click();
  document.body.removeChild(a);
  URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
}

================================================================================
INICIO DE ARCHIVO: src/services/screenshotBlockService.ts
================================================================================

import { registerPlugin } from '@capacitor/core';
import { isNativeApp } from './nativeVaultBridge';

interface VaultSecurityPluginInterface {
  enableScreenshotBlock(): Promise<{ success: boolean }>;
  disableScreenshotBlock(): Promise<{ success: boolean }>;
}

const NativeVaultSecurity = registerPlugin<VaultSecurityPluginInterface>('VaultSecurity');

/**
 * Servicio de Bloqueo de Capturas y Protección de Pantalla de NullVault
 * - Bloquea atajos de teclado de captura (PrintScreen, Win+Shift+S, Cmd+Shift+3/4/5, Ctrl+P)
 * - Muestra la leyenda de seguridad requerida: "Captura no permitida. Para desactivar, ve a Seguridad e introduce tu PIN"
 * - Vacía el portapapeles ante cualquier intento de snapshot
 * - Desactiva menú contextual, descarga directa y arrastre de medios
 * - Oculta todo el contenido en impresión (@media print)
 */

let isBlockerActive = false;
let toastCallback: ((msg: string, type?: 'info' | 'success' | 'alert' | 'error') => void) | null = null;
let lastAlertTime = 0;

export const setScreenshotBlockToastCallback = (
  cb: (msg: string, type?: 'info' | 'success' | 'alert' | 'error') => void
) => {
  toastCallback = cb;
};

export const triggerScreenshotBlockedAlert = () => {
  const now = Date.now();
  if (now - lastAlertTime < 1500) return; // Debounce alerts
  lastAlertTime = now;

  // Clear clipboard immediately to prevent saving any screen snip
  try {
    if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) {
      navigator.clipboard.writeText('');
    }
  } catch {}

  // Show detailed security notice
  if (toastCallback) {
    toastCallback(
      '🛡️ Captura de pantalla no permitida por seguridad de la Bóveda. Si deseas desactivar el bloqueo, ve a la pestaña Seguridad e introduce tu PIN.',
      'alert'
    );
  }

  // Dispatch custom event for any listeners
  window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('nullvault_screenshot_attempt_blocked'));
};

const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
  if (!isBlockerActive) return;

  const key = e.key || '';
  const code = e.code || '';

  // 1. PrintScreen (Key code 44 / PrintScreen)
  if (key === 'PrintScreen' || code === 'PrintScreen' || e.keyCode === 44) {
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopPropagation();
    triggerScreenshotBlockedAlert();
    return;
  }

  // 2. Ctrl+P / Cmd+P (Print to PDF / save page)
  if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && (key === 'p' || key === 'P')) {
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopPropagation();
    triggerScreenshotBlockedAlert();
    return;
  }

  // 3. Windows Snipping Tool (Win+Shift+S), Mac Screenshot (Cmd+Shift+3/4/5), Chrome Screenshot (Ctrl+Shift+S)
  if ((e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey) && e.shiftKey) {
    if (['3', '4', '5', 's', 'S', 'c', 'C', 'i', 'I', 'j', 'J'].includes(key)) {
      e.preventDefault();
      e.stopPropagation();
      triggerScreenshotBlockedAlert();
      return;
    }
  }

  // 4. Alt + PrintScreen
  if (e.altKey && (key === 'PrintScreen' || code === 'PrintScreen' || e.keyCode === 44)) {
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopPropagation();
    triggerScreenshotBlockedAlert();
    return;
  }
};

const handleKeyUp = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
  if (!isBlockerActive) return;
  const key = e.key || '';
  const code = e.code || '';

  if (key === 'PrintScreen' || code === 'PrintScreen' || e.keyCode === 44) {
    e.preventDefault();
    triggerScreenshotBlockedAlert();
  }
};

const handleContextMenu = (e: MouseEvent) => {
  if (!isBlockerActive) return;
  e.preventDefault();
  triggerScreenshotBlockedAlert();
};

const handleDragStart = (e: DragEvent) => {
  if (!isBlockerActive) return;
  e.preventDefault();
};

const handleSelectStart = (e: Event) => {
  if (!isBlockerActive) return;
  // Permitir selección solo dentro de inputs o textareas
  const target = e.target as HTMLElement;
  if (target && (target.tagName === 'INPUT' || target.tagName === 'TEXTAREA')) {
    return;
  }
  e.preventDefault();
};

export const initScreenshotBlocker = () => {
  const isEnabled = localStorage.getItem('vault_screenshot_block_active') !== 'false';
  setScreenshotBlockEnabled(isEnabled);

  window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown, true);
  window.addEventListener('keyup', handleKeyUp, true);
  window.addEventListener('contextmenu', handleContextMenu, true);
  window.addEventListener('dragstart', handleDragStart, true);
  window.addEventListener('selectstart', handleSelectStart, true);

  // Escuchar cambios de configuración desde la pestaña de seguridad
  window.addEventListener('vault_security_settings_changed', () => {
    const updated = localStorage.getItem('vault_screenshot_block_active') !== 'false';
    setScreenshotBlockEnabled(updated);
  });
};

export const setScreenshotBlockEnabled = (enabled: boolean) => {
  isBlockerActive = enabled;

  // Inyectar o remover estilos de protección anti-captura
  let styleEl = document.getElementById('nullvault-screenshot-protection-style');
  if (enabled) {
    if (!styleEl) {
      styleEl = document.createElement('style');
      styleEl.id = 'nullvault-screenshot-protection-style';
      styleEl.innerHTML = `
        @media print {
          html, body, #root, * {
            display: none !important;
            visibility: hidden !important;
            opacity: 0 !important;
          }
        }
        .nullvault-protected-screen {
          -webkit-touch-callout: none !important;
          -webkit-user-select: none !important;
          -moz-user-select: none !important;
          -ms-user-select: none !important;
          user-select: none !important;
        }
        .nullvault-protected-screen img,
        .nullvault-protected-screen video,
        .nullvault-protected-screen canvas {
          -webkit-user-drag: none !important;
          -webkit-touch-callout: none !important;
          user-select: none !important;
          pointer-events: auto;
        }
      `;
      document.head.appendChild(styleEl);
    }
    document.body.classList.add('nullvault-protected-screen');

    // Activar protección nativa si es Android APK (FLAG_SECURE a nivel de sistema)
    try {
      if (isNativeApp()) {
        NativeVaultSecurity.enableScreenshotBlock().catch(() => {});
      }
    } catch {}
  } else {
    if (styleEl) {
      styleEl.remove();
    }
    document.body.classList.remove('nullvault-protected-screen');

    try {
      if (isNativeApp()) {
        NativeVaultSecurity.disableScreenshotBlock().catch(() => {});
      }
    } catch {}
  }
};

================================================================================
INICIO DE ARCHIVO: src/services/updateService.ts

/**
 * Servicio de detección, notificación y aplicación de actualizaciones para la aplicación.
 */

import { CURRENT_APP_VERSION, CURRENT_BUILD_NUMBER, CURRENT_RELEASE_DATE, CURRENT_CHANGELOG, VersionInfo } from '../version';

export interface UpdateCheckResult {
  hasUpdate: boolean;
  currentVersion: string;
  latestVersion: string;
  releaseDate?: string;
  changelog?: string[];
  mandatory?: boolean;
  isServiceWorkerReady?: boolean;
  downloadUrl?: string;
  error?: string;
}

type UpdateListener = (result: UpdateCheckResult) => void;

class UpdateService {
  private listeners: Set<UpdateListener> = new Set();
  private lastCheckResult: UpdateCheckResult | null = null;
  private isChecking: boolean = false;

  constructor() {
    this.initServiceWorkerListener();
  }

  /**
   * Escucha si el service worker detecta un nuevo bundle listo en segundo plano.
   */
  private initServiceWorkerListener() {
    if (typeof window === 'undefined' || !('serviceWorker' in navigator)) return;

    navigator.serviceWorker.addEventListener('controllerchange', () => {
      // El nuevo worker ha tomado control
      console.log('[UpdateService] Controller cambiado, nuevo SW activo');
    });
  }

  /**
   * Compara dos versiones semver (ej. "1.2.0" y "1.2.1").
   * Retorna 1 si v1 > v2, -1 si v1 < v2, y 0 si son iguales.
   */
  public compareVersions(v1: string, v2: string): number {
    const clean1 = v1.replace(/^v/, '').split('.').map(Number);
    const clean2 = v2.replace(/^v/, '').split('.').map(Number);

    for (let i = 0; i < Math.max(clean1.length, clean2.length); i++) {
      const num1 = clean1[i] || 0;
      const num2 = clean2[i] || 0;
      if (num1 > num2) return 1;
      if (num1 < num2) return -1;
    }
    return 0;
  }

  /**
   * Comprueba si hay una actualización disponible consultando version.json y el Service Worker.
   */
  public async checkForUpdates(force: boolean = false): Promise<UpdateCheckResult> {
    if (this.isChecking) {
      return this.lastCheckResult || {
        hasUpdate: false,
        currentVersion: CURRENT_APP_VERSION,
        latestVersion: CURRENT_APP_VERSION,
      };
    }

    this.isChecking = true;

    try {
      // 1. Verificar si hay un Service Worker esperando (update pendiente de PWA)
      let swHasUpdate = false;
      if ('serviceWorker' in navigator) {
        try {
          const reg = await navigator.serviceWorker.getRegistration();
          if (reg) {
            await reg.update();
            if (reg.waiting) {
              swHasUpdate = true;
            }
          }
        } catch (swErr) {
          console.warn('[UpdateService] No se pudo verificar ServiceWorker:', swErr);
        }
      }

      // 2. Determinar la fuente de comprobación (Local / Servidor web o Remoto de GitHub)
      const cacheBuster = `_t=${Date.now()}`;
      const githubRepo = this.getGitHubRepo();
      let remoteData: VersionInfo | null = null;
      let downloadUrl = '';

      // Si hay un repositorio de GitHub configurado, consultamos la versión más reciente en GitHub
      if (githubRepo) {
        try {
          const ghUrl = `https://raw.githubusercontent.com/${githubRepo}/main/public/version.json?${cacheBuster}`;
          const ghRes = await fetch(ghUrl, { cache: 'no-store' });
          if (ghRes.ok) {
            remoteData = await ghRes.json();
            downloadUrl = `https://github.com/${githubRepo}/actions`;
          }
        } catch (ghErr) {
          console.warn('[UpdateService] No se pudo consultar GitHub remoto, probando ruta local:', ghErr);
        }
      }

      // Si no se obtuvo de GitHub, consultamos la ruta local/web /version.json
      if (!remoteData) {
        const response = await fetch(`/version.json?${cacheBuster}`, {
          cache: 'no-store',
          headers: {
            'Cache-Control': 'no-cache, no-store, must-revalidate',
            'Pragma': 'no-cache',
          },
        });

        if (response.ok) {
          remoteData = await response.json();
        }
      }

      if (!remoteData) {
        throw new Error('No se pudo obtener información de versión del servidor ni de GitHub');
      }

      const versionDiff = this.compareVersions(remoteData.version, CURRENT_APP_VERSION);
      const buildDiff = (remoteData.buildNumber || 0) > CURRENT_BUILD_NUMBER;

      const hasRemoteUpdate = versionDiff > 0 || buildDiff;
      const hasUpdate = hasRemoteUpdate || swHasUpdate;

      const result: UpdateCheckResult = {
        hasUpdate,
        currentVersion: CURRENT_APP_VERSION,
        latestVersion: hasRemoteUpdate ? remoteData.version : (swHasUpdate ? CURRENT_APP_VERSION : CURRENT_APP_VERSION),
        releaseDate: remoteData.releaseDate || CURRENT_RELEASE_DATE,
        changelog: remoteData.changelog || CURRENT_CHANGELOG,
        mandatory: remoteData.mandatory || false,
        isServiceWorkerReady: swHasUpdate,
        downloadUrl: downloadUrl || (githubRepo ? `https://github.com/${githubRepo}/actions` : undefined),
      };

      this.lastCheckResult = result;
      localStorage.setItem('vault_last_update_check', Date.now().toString());

      if (hasUpdate) {
        this.notifyListeners(result);
      }

      return result;
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.warn('[UpdateService] Error al verificar actualizaciones:', err);
      const fallbackResult: UpdateCheckResult = {
        hasUpdate: false,
        currentVersion: CURRENT_APP_VERSION,
        latestVersion: CURRENT_APP_VERSION,
        error: err?.message || 'No se pudo conectar al servidor de actualizaciones',
      };
      this.lastCheckResult = fallbackResult;
      return fallbackResult;
    } finally {
      this.isChecking = false;
    }
  }

  /**
   * Aplica la actualización recargando la aplicación y liberando cachés de forma limpia.
   */
  public async applyUpdate(): Promise<void> {
    try {
      sessionStorage.setItem('vault_just_updated', 'true');
      sessionStorage.setItem('vault_updated_version', CURRENT_APP_VERSION);

      // 1. Enviar SKIP_WAITING a los Service Workers en espera
      if ('serviceWorker' in navigator) {
        const reg = await navigator.serviceWorker.getRegistration();
        if (reg?.waiting) {
          reg.waiting.postMessage({ type: 'SKIP_WAITING' });
        }
      }

      // 2. Limpiar caches del navegador para garantizar assets frescos
      if ('caches' in window) {
        try {
          const keys = await caches.keys();
          await Promise.all(keys.map((k) => caches.delete(k)));
        } catch (cErr) {
          console.warn('[UpdateService] Error al limpiar caches:', cErr);
        }
      }

      // 3. Breve pausa para asegurar propagación y recargar
      setTimeout(() => {
        window.location.reload();
      }, 250);
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('[UpdateService] Error al aplicar actualización:', e);
      window.location.reload();
    }
  }

  /**
   * Suscribirse a avisos de actualización
   */
  public subscribe(listener: UpdateListener): () => void {
    this.listeners.add(listener);
    if (this.lastCheckResult && this.lastCheckResult.hasUpdate) {
      listener(this.lastCheckResult);
    }
    return () => {
      this.listeners.delete(listener);
    };
  }

  private notifyListeners(result: UpdateCheckResult) {
    this.listeners.forEach((listener) => {
      try {
        listener(result);
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('[UpdateService] Error en listener:', err);
      }
    });
  }

  /**
   * Verifica si el usuario activó la comprobación automática al entrar
   */
  public isAutoCheckEnabled(): boolean {
    const saved = localStorage.getItem('vault_auto_check_updates');
    return saved !== null ? saved === 'true' : true;
  }

  public setAutoCheckEnabled(enabled: boolean): void {
    localStorage.setItem('vault_auto_check_updates', enabled ? 'true' : 'false');
  }

  /**
   * Gestión de versión pospuesta / descartada para no molestar continuamente
   */
  public isUpdateDismissed(version: string): boolean {
    const dismissed = localStorage.getItem('vault_dismissed_update_version');
    return dismissed === version;
  }

  public dismissUpdate(version: string): void {
    localStorage.setItem('vault_dismissed_update_version', version);
  }

  public clearDismissedUpdate(): void {
    localStorage.removeItem('vault_dismissed_update_version');
  }

  public getLastCheckTime(): number | null {
    const t = localStorage.getItem('vault_last_update_check');
    return t ? Number(t) : null;
  }

  public getLastCheckResult(): UpdateCheckResult | null {
    return this.lastCheckResult;
  }

  public getGitHubRepo(): string {
    return localStorage.getItem('vault_github_repo') || 'cuentadecuperacion7525-hub/Mi-B-veda.-';
  }

  public setGitHubRepo(repo: string): void {
    const cleaned = repo.trim().replace(/^https?:\/\/github\.com\//, '').replace(/\/$/, '');
    if (cleaned) {
      localStorage.setItem('vault_github_repo', cleaned);
    } else {
      localStorage.removeItem('vault_github_repo');
    }
  }
}

export const updateService = new UpdateService();
