                  onChange={(e) => setBackupPinInput(e.target.value.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, 8))}
                  placeholder="••••"
                  className="w-full bg-[#162238] border border-slate-800 focus:border-emerald-500 rounded-xl px-4 py-2.5 text-center text-lg font-bold text-white tracking-widest focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-emerald-500 font-mono"
                  disabled={isVerifyingBackupPin}
                  autoFocus
                />
              </div>

              {backupPinError && (
                <p className="text-[11px] text-rose-400 font-medium text-center bg-rose-500/10 py-2 px-3 rounded-xl border border-rose-500/20">
                  {backupPinError}
                </p>
              )}

              <div className="flex gap-3">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    setShowBackupPinPrompt(false);
                    setPendingBackupBlob(null);
                    setPendingBackupHeader(null);
                  }}
                  className="flex-1 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-300 text-xs font-semibold transition-colors"
                  disabled={isVerifyingBackupPin}
                >
                  Cancelar
                </button>
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  disabled={backupPinInput.length < 4 || isVerifyingBackupPin}
                  className="flex-1 py-2 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white text-xs font-semibold transition-colors flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5"
                >
                  <span>{isVerifyingBackupPin ? 'Verificando...' : 'Restaurar'}</span>
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
      {/* Modal de Reinicio Requerido tras Restaurar Copia */}
      {restartPromptInfo.show && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[70] bg-black/90 backdrop-blur-xl flex items-center justify-center p-4 animate-in fade-in">
          <div className="w-full max-w-sm bg-[#0a121d] border-2 border-emerald-500/60 rounded-3xl p-6 shadow-2xl space-y-5 text-center animate-in zoom-in-95 duration-200">
            <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-3xl bg-emerald-500/20 border-2 border-emerald-500/50 text-emerald-400 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto shadow-xl shadow-emerald-500/20">
              <CheckCircle2 className="w-8 h-8" />
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-2">
              <h3 className="text-lg font-black text-white tracking-tight">
                ¡Copia de Seguridad Restaurada!
              </h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-emerald-300 font-semibold bg-emerald-500/10 py-1.5 px-3 rounded-full inline-block border border-emerald-500/30">
                Se restauraron {restartPromptInfo.importedCount} archivos con éxito
              </p>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-300 pt-1 leading-relaxed">
                Para aplicar todos los cambios de forma segura, sincronizar las claves criptográficas y recargar todas tus fotos y videos en la bóveda, <strong className="text-white">es necesario reiniciar la aplicación</strong>.
              </p>
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-2.5 pt-1">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  window.location.reload();
                }}
                className="w-full py-3.5 bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-500 via-teal-500 to-cyan-500 hover:from-emerald-400 hover:to-cyan-400 text-slate-950 font-black rounded-2xl text-sm transition-all shadow-xl shadow-emerald-500/25 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 cursor-pointer active:scale-98"
              >
                <span>🔄 Reiniciar Aplicación Ahora</span>
              </button>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={async () => {
                  setRestartPromptInfo({ show: false, importedCount: 0 });
                  await onPhotosUpdated();
                }}
                className="w-full py-2.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-white text-xs font-semibold rounded-xl hover:bg-slate-800/50 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
              >
                Cerrar y continuar sin reiniciar
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Modal de Novedades y Actualizaciones */}
      <UpdateNotificationModal
        isOpen={showChangelogModal}
        onClose={() => setShowChangelogModal(false)}
        updateInfo={updateCheckResult}
        viewNotesOnly={!updateCheckResult?.hasUpdate}
      />
    </div>
  );
};


================================================================================
FIN DE ARCHIVO: src/components/SettingsVaultView.tsx
================================================================================


================================================================================
INICIO DE ARCHIVO: src/components/SetupVaultView.tsx
================================================================================

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import {
  ShieldCheck,
  KeyRound,
  AlertCircle,
  Sparkles,
  Copy,
  Check,
  Download,
  CheckCircle2,
  Lock,
  Loader2,
  ShieldAlert,
} from 'lucide-react';
import {
  deriveKeyFromPin,
  generateSalt,
  createVerificationPayload,
  bufToHex,
} from '../crypto/vaultCrypto';
import {
  generateRecoveryKey,
  backupMasterKeyWithRecoveryKey,
  downloadRecoveryKeyFile,
  formatRecoveryKeyDisplay,
} from '../services/recoveryKeyService';
import { PinKeypad } from './PinKeypad';

interface SetupVaultViewProps {
  onSetupComplete: (masterKey: CryptoKey, pin?: string) => void;
  showToast: (msg: string, type?: 'info' | 'success' | 'alert' | 'error') => void;
}

export const SetupVaultView: React.FC<SetupVaultViewProps> = ({
  onSetupComplete,
  showToast,
}) => {
  const [step, setStep] = useState<'create' | 'confirm' | 'recovery_key'>('create');
  const [pin, setPin] = useState('');
  const [confirmPin, setConfirmPin] = useState('');
  const [isProcessing, setIsProcessing] = useState(false);
  const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Recovery Key states
  const [recoveryKey, setRecoveryKey] = useState('');
  const [copiedKey, setCopiedKey] = useState(false);
  const [downloadedKey, setDownloadedKey] = useState(false);
  const [savedConfirmed, setSavedConfirmed] = useState(false);

  const handleBackToCreate = () => {
    setStep('create');
    setConfirmPin('');
    setErrorMessage(null);
  };

  // Called when PIN is confirmed
  const handlePinConfirmed = (confirmedValue: string) => {
    if (confirmedValue !== pin) {
      setErrorMessage('Los PINs no coinciden. Inténtalo de nuevo.');
      setConfirmPin('');
      return;
    }
    setErrorMessage(null);
    // Generate the master recovery key and display it
    const newKey = generateRecoveryKey();
    setRecoveryKey(newKey);
    setStep('recovery_key');
  };

  // Copies recovery key to clipboard
  const handleCopyKey = () => {
    try {
      navigator.clipboard.writeText(recoveryKey);
      setCopiedKey(true);
      setSavedConfirmed(true);
      showToast('¡Clave de recuperación copiada al portapapeles!', 'success');
      setTimeout(() => setCopiedKey(false), 3000);
    } catch {
      showToast('Selecciona y copia el texto manualmente', 'info');
    }
  };

  // Downloads text backup file
  const handleDownloadKey = () => {
    try {
      downloadRecoveryKeyFile(recoveryKey);
      setDownloadedKey(true);
      setSavedConfirmed(true);
      showToast('Archivo de clave de recuperación descargado', 'success');
    } catch (e) {
      console.error(e);
      showToast('Error al descargar archivo de respaldo', 'error');
    }
  };

  // Finalizes vault cryptographic storage and unlocks
  const finalizeSetup = async () => {
    try {
      setIsProcessing(true);
      setErrorMessage(null);

      // 1. Generate cryptographic salt and derive AES-GCM 256 master key
      const salt = generateSalt();
      const saltHex = bufToHex(salt);
      const masterKey = await deriveKeyFromPin(pin, salt);

      // 2. Create test verification token
      const verification = await createVerificationPayload(masterKey);

      // 3. Store vault state in localStorage
      localStorage.setItem('vault_salt', saltHex);
      localStorage.setItem('vault_test_iv', verification.ivHex);
      localStorage.setItem('vault_test_cipher', verification.cipherHex);
      localStorage.setItem('vault_initialized', 'true');
      localStorage.setItem('vault_pin_length', pin.length.toString());

      try {
        sessionStorage.setItem('vault_session_pin', pin);
      } catch {}

      // 4. Wrap and backup master key with the recovery key
      if (recoveryKey) {
        await backupMasterKeyWithRecoveryKey(masterKey, recoveryKey);
      }

      showToast('¡Bóveda configurada y clave de respaldo activada!', 'success');
      onSetupComplete(masterKey, pin);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Error creating vault:', err);
      setErrorMessage('Error al inicializar la bóveda criptográfica');
      setIsProcessing(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#090d16] flex flex-col justify-center items-center p-4">
      {/* Background ambient glow */}
      <div className="fixed inset-0 pointer-events-none flex justify-center items-center overflow-hidden">
        <div className="w-[500px] h-[500px] bg-indigo-600/10 rounded-full blur-[120px]" />
      </div>

      <div className="relative w-full max-w-md bg-[#131c2e]/90 border border-slate-800/90 rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 shadow-2xl backdrop-blur-xl text-center">
        {/* Header Icon */}
        <div className="w-16 h-16 bg-indigo-500/10 border border-indigo-500/30 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-5 text-indigo-400 shadow-inner shadow-indigo-500/20">
          {step === 'create' ? (
            <KeyRound className="w-8 h-8" />
          ) : step === 'confirm' ? (
            <ShieldCheck className="w-8 h-8 text-emerald-400" />
          ) : (
            <Lock className="w-8 h-8 text-[#00e5ff]" />
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Titles and Descriptions according to step */}
        {step === 'create' && (
          <>
            <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-white mb-2">
              Configura tu Bóveda
            </h1>
            <p className="text-sm text-slate-400 mb-6 leading-relaxed">
              Crea un PIN numérico de 4 dígitos. Tus fotos, videos y documentos se cifrarán localmente mediante AES-GCM de 256 bits.
            </p>
          </>
        )}

        {step === 'confirm' && (
          <>
            <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-white mb-2">
              Confirma tu PIN
            </h1>
            <p className="text-sm text-slate-400 mb-6 leading-relaxed">
              Ingresa de nuevo el mismo PIN maestro para confirmar la clave de cifrado.
            </p>
          </>
        )}

        {step === 'recovery_key' && (
          <>
            <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1 rounded-full bg-[#00e5ff]/10 border border-[#00e5ff]/30 text-[#00e5ff] text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider mb-2">
              <ShieldCheck className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>Respaldo de Emergencia</span>
            </div>
            <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-white mb-2">
              Tu Clave de <span className="text-[#00e5ff]">Respaldo Maestra</span>
            </h1>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mb-5 leading-relaxed">
              Guarda esta clave en un lugar seguro. Si alguna vez olvidas tu PIN, esta clave es la que te permitirá recuperarlo <strong>sin perder ninguna de tus fotos</strong>.
            </p>
          </>
        )}

        {/* Step indicator */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 mb-6">
          <span
            className={`h-1.5 rounded-full transition-all duration-300 ${
              step === 'create' ? 'w-8 bg-indigo-500' : 'w-2 bg-slate-700'
            }`}
          />
          <span
            className={`h-1.5 rounded-full transition-all duration-300 ${
              step === 'confirm' ? 'w-8 bg-indigo-500' : 'w-2 bg-slate-700'
            }`}
          />
          <span
            className={`h-1.5 rounded-full transition-all duration-300 ${
              step === 'recovery_key' ? 'w-8 bg-[#00e5ff]' : 'w-2 bg-slate-700'
            }`}
          />
        </div>

        {/* STEP 1: CREATE PIN */}
        {step === 'create' && (
          <div>
            <PinKeypad
              pin={pin}
              onPinChange={(val) => {
                setPin(val);
                setErrorMessage(null);
                if (val.length === 4) {
                  setTimeout(() => {
                    setStep('confirm');
                  }, 120);
                }
              }}
              maxDigits={4}
              disabled={isProcessing}
            />

            {errorMessage && (
              <div className="mt-4 flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 text-rose-400 text-xs bg-rose-500/10 py-2 px-3 rounded-xl border border-rose-500/20">
                <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />
                <span>{errorMessage}</span>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* STEP 2: CONFIRM PIN */}
        {step === 'confirm' && (
          <div>
            <PinKeypad
              pin={confirmPin}
              onPinChange={(val) => {
                setConfirmPin(val);
                setErrorMessage(null);
                if (val.length === 4) {
                  handlePinConfirmed(val);
                }
              }}
              maxDigits={4}
              disabled={isProcessing}
            />

            {errorMessage && (
              <div className="mt-4 flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 text-rose-400 text-xs bg-rose-500/10 py-2 px-3 rounded-xl border border-rose-500/20">
                <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />
                <span>{errorMessage}</span>
              </div>
            )}

            <div className="mt-5 flex justify-center">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleBackToCreate}
                disabled={isProcessing}
                className="py-2.5 px-6 bg-slate-800/80 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-300 text-xs font-medium rounded-xl border border-slate-700/80 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
              >
                Volver a cambiar PIN
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* STEP 3: RECOVERY KEY DISPLAY */}
        {step === 'recovery_key' && (
          <div className="space-y-4 text-left">
            {/* Clave Card */}
            <div className="bg-[#0b101d] border-2 border-[#00e5ff]/40 rounded-2xl p-4 shadow-xl shadow-cyan-500/10 text-center">
              <p className="text-[10px] uppercase font-bold tracking-widest text-[#00e5ff] mb-1.5">
                Clave de Seguridad Personal (16 dígitos)
              </p>
              <div className="py-2 px-3 bg-black/60 rounded-xl border border-slate-800">
                <span className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-black font-mono tracking-widest text-white selection:bg-[#00e5ff] selection:text-black">
                  {formatRecoveryKeyDisplay(recoveryKey)}
                </span>
              </div>

              {/* Action Buttons: Copiar y Descargar */}
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 mt-3">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={handleCopyKey}
                  className="flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 py-2.5 px-3 bg-[#00e5ff]/20 hover:bg-[#00e5ff]/30 text-[#00e5ff] text-xs font-semibold rounded-xl border border-[#00e5ff]/40 transition-colors cursor-pointer active:scale-95"
                >
                  {copiedKey ? (
                    <>
                      <Check className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-400" />
                      <span>¡Copiada!</span>
                    </>
                  ) : (
                    <>
                      <Copy className="w-4 h-4" />
                      <span>Copiar Clave</span>
                    </>
                  )}
                </button>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={handleDownloadKey}
                  className="flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 py-2.5 px-3 bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-200 text-xs font-semibold rounded-xl border border-slate-700 transition-colors cursor-pointer active:scale-95"
                >
                  {downloadedKey ? (
                    <>
                      <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-400" />
                      <span>Descargado</span>
                    </>
                  ) : (
                    <>
                      <Download className="w-4 h-4" />
                      <span>Descargar .txt</span>
                    </>
                  )}
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Checkbox confirmation */}
            <label className="flex items-start gap-2.5 p-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-900/60 border border-slate-800/80 cursor-pointer select-none">
              <input
                type="checkbox"
                checked={savedConfirmed}
                onChange={(e) => setSavedConfirmed(e.target.checked)}
                className="mt-0.5 w-4 h-4 rounded text-[#00e5ff] border-slate-700 focus:ring-0 focus:ring-offset-0 bg-slate-800 accent-[#00e5ff] cursor-pointer"
              />
              <span className="text-xs text-slate-300 leading-snug">
                He copiado, anotado o guardado esta clave en un lugar seguro. Entiendo que es necesaria si olvido mi PIN.
              </span>
            </label>

            {errorMessage && (
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-rose-400 text-xs bg-rose-500/10 p-3 rounded-xl border border-rose-500/20">
                <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />
                <span>{errorMessage}</span>
              </div>
            )}

            {/* Main Action Button */}
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={finalizeSetup}
              disabled={isProcessing || (!savedConfirmed && !copiedKey && !downloadedKey)}
              className="w-full py-3.5 bg-gradient-to-r from-[#00e5ff] to-blue-600 hover:from-cyan-400 hover:to-blue-500 disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed text-slate-950 font-bold rounded-xl text-sm transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-lg shadow-cyan-500/20 cursor-pointer active:scale-95"
            >
              {isProcessing ? (
                <>
                  <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin" />
                  <span>Inicializando bóveda segura...</span>
                </>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4" />
                  <span>Finalizar y Entrar a la Bóveda</span>
                </>
              )}
            </button>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Security Info Footnote */}
        <div className="mt-6 pt-5 border-t border-slate-800/80 text-left flex items-start gap-2.5 text-xs text-slate-400">
          <Sparkles className="w-4 h-4 text-indigo-400 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
          <p>
            {step === 'recovery_key'
              ? 'Cero dependencias externas: tu clave de respaldo funciona 100% sin conexión a internet y protege tus fotos cifradas con AES-GCM de 256 bits.'
              : 'Cifrado de conocimiento cero: tu PIN nunca sale de este dispositivo. Tus archivos están protegidos con AES-GCM 256 bits.'}
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

================================================================================
INICIO DE ARCHIVO: src/components/Toast.tsx
================================================================================

import React from 'react';
import { CheckCircle2, AlertTriangle, AlertCircle, Info, Lock } from 'lucide-react';

export interface ToastData {
  id: string;
  message: string;
  type: 'info' | 'success' | 'alert' | 'error';
}

interface ToastProps {
  toasts: ToastData[];
}

export const ToastContainer: React.FC<ToastProps> = ({ toasts }) => {
  if (toasts.length === 0) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed top-5 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 z-70 flex flex-col gap-2 pointer-events-none w-full max-w-sm px-4">
      {toasts.map((t) => {
        let Icon = Info;
        let colorClass = 'text-indigo-400 border-indigo-500/30';

        if (t.type === 'success') {
          Icon = CheckCircle2;
          colorClass = 'text-emerald-400 border-emerald-500/30';
        } else if (t.type === 'alert') {
          Icon = AlertTriangle;
          colorClass = 'text-amber-400 border-amber-500/30';
        } else if (t.type === 'error') {
          Icon = AlertCircle;
          colorClass = 'text-rose-400 border-rose-500/30';
        }

        return (
          <div
            key={t.id}
            className={`bg-[#131c2e]/95 border ${colorClass} text-slate-100 px-4 py-3 rounded-2xl shadow-2xl backdrop-blur-xl flex items-center gap-3 text-xs font-medium animate-in fade-in slide-in-from-top-3 duration-200`}
          >
            <Icon className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />
            <span className="flex-1 leading-snug">{t.message}</span>
          </div>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
};

FIN DE ARCHIVO: src/components/Toast.tsx

================================================================================
INICIO DE ARCHIVO: src/components/UpdateNotificationModal.tsx
================================================================================

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { 
  Sparkles, 
  RefreshCw, 
  ArrowUpCircle, 
  CheckCircle2, 
  X, 
  ChevronRight, 
  ShieldCheck, 
  Calendar,
  Layers
} from 'lucide-react';
import { updateService, UpdateCheckResult } from '../services/updateService';
import { CURRENT_APP_VERSION, CURRENT_RELEASE_DATE, CURRENT_CHANGELOG } from '../version';

interface UpdateNotificationModalProps {
  // Cuando se detecta una nueva actualización
  updateInfo: UpdateCheckResult | null;
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  // Si se abre solo para consultar las novedades de la versión actual
  viewNotesOnly?: boolean;
}

export const UpdateNotificationModal: React.FC<UpdateNotificationModalProps> = ({
  updateInfo,
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  viewNotesOnly = false,
}) => {
  const [isApplying, setIsApplying] = useState(false);
  const [showFullChangelog, setShowFullChangelog] = useState(!viewNotesOnly);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const targetVersion = viewNotesOnly 
    ? CURRENT_APP_VERSION 
    : (updateInfo?.latestVersion || CURRENT_APP_VERSION);

  const releaseDate = viewNotesOnly 
    ? CURRENT_RELEASE_DATE 
    : (updateInfo?.releaseDate || CURRENT_RELEASE_DATE);

  const changelog = (viewNotesOnly ? CURRENT_CHANGELOG : updateInfo?.changelog) || CURRENT_CHANGELOG;

  const handleApplyUpdate = async () => {
    setIsApplying(true);
    try {
      if (updateInfo?.downloadUrl) {
        window.open(updateInfo.downloadUrl, '_blank');
        setIsApplying(false);
        onClose();
        return;
      }
      await updateService.applyUpdate();
    } catch {
      window.location.reload();
    }
  };

  const handleDismiss = () => {
    if (!viewNotesOnly && updateInfo?.latestVersion) {
      updateService.dismissUpdate(updateInfo.latestVersion);
    }
    onClose();
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[10000] bg-black/85 backdrop-blur-md flex items-center justify-center p-4 animate-in fade-in duration-200">
      <div 
        className="w-full max-w-md bg-[#0a121d] border border-cyan-500/40 rounded-3xl p-5 sm:p-6 shadow-[0_0_50px_rgba(0,229,255,0.18)] relative flex flex-col max-h-[90vh] animate-in zoom-in-95 duration-200"
      >
        {/* Botón cerrar */}
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={handleDismiss}
          disabled={isApplying}
          className="absolute top-4 right-4 w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 text-slate-400 hover:text-white flex items-center justify-center transition-colors"
          title="Cerrar"
        >
          <X className="w-4 h-4" />
        </button>

        {/* Encabezado del aviso */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3.5 mb-4 pr-8">
          <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl bg-cyan-500/15 border border-cyan-500/30 text-[#00e5ff] flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 shadow-lg shadow-cyan-500/10">
            {viewNotesOnly ? (
              <ShieldCheck className="w-6 h-6 stroke-[2]" />
            ) : (
              <Sparkles className="w-6 h-6 stroke-[2] animate-pulse" />
            )}
          </div>
          <div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-cyan-500/20 text-[#00e5ff] border border-cyan-500/30">
                {viewNotesOnly ? 'Versión Instalada' : 'Nueva Actualización'}
              </span>
              <span className="font-mono text-xs font-bold text-white">
                v{targetVersion}
              </span>
            </div>
            <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-white mt-1">
              {viewNotesOnly 
                ? 'Novedades y Mejoras' 
                : '¡Nueva Versión Lista!'}
            </h3>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Resumen o mensaje */}
        <p className="text-xs text-slate-300 leading-relaxed mb-4">
          {viewNotesOnly
            ? 'Tu bóveda cuenta con las optimizaciones más recientes para navegación, seguridad y visualización multimedia.'
            : 'Se han implementado mejoras importantes en el desplazamiento, zoom táctil y rendimiento general. Tus fotos y configuraciones permanecen 100% protegidas e intactas.'}
        </p>

        {/* Lista de cambios (Changelog) con scroll */}
        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto space-y-2.5 bg-slate-900/80 border border-slate-800/90 rounded-2xl p-3.5 mb-5 max-h-[42vh] custom-scrollbar">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] text-slate-400 font-medium pb-2 border-b border-slate-800">
            <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-slate-300 font-semibold">
              <Layers className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#00e5ff]" />
              Cambios incluidos:
            </span>
            <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-[10px] text-slate-500">
              <Calendar className="w-3 h-3" />
              {releaseDate}
            </span>
          </div>

          <ul className="space-y-2.5 pt-1">
            {changelog.map((item, index) => (
              <li key={index} className="flex items-start gap-2.5 text-xs text-slate-200 leading-snug">
                <div className="w-4 h-4 rounded-full bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-400 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 mt-0.5 border border-emerald-500/30">
                  <CheckCircle2 className="w-3 h-3 stroke-[2.5]" />
                </div>
                <span>{item}</span>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </div>

        {/* Botones de acción */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 pt-1">
          {viewNotesOnly ? (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              className="w-full py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-white font-bold text-xs transition-colors flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
            >
              <span>Entendido</span>
            </button>
          ) : (
            <>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleDismiss}
                disabled={isApplying}
                className="flex-1 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-800 text-slate-300 font-semibold text-xs transition-colors"
              >
                Más tarde
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleApplyUpdate}
                disabled={isApplying}
                className="flex-1 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-cyan-500 to-blue-600 hover:from-cyan-400 hover:to-blue-500 text-white font-bold text-xs transition-all shadow-lg shadow-cyan-500/25 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 active:scale-[0.98]"
              >
                {isApplying ? (
                  <>
                    <RefreshCw className="w-3.5 h-3.5 animate-spin text-white" />
                    <span>Actualizando...</span>
                  </>
                ) : (
                  <>
                    <RefreshCw className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-white" />
                    <span>Actualizar Ahora</span>
                  </>
                )}
              </button>
            </>
          )}
        </div>

        <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 text-center mt-3">
          {viewNotesOnly
            ? 'Cifrado permanente AES-GCM 256 bits activo.'
            : 'La actualización se aplica al instante sin cerrar tu sesión.'}
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

/**
 * Banner superior discreto que aparece al abrir la app si hay actualización lista
 */
interface UpdateBannerProps {
  updateInfo: UpdateCheckResult;
  onOpenDetails: () => void;
  onApplyUpdate: () => void;
  onDismiss: () => void;
}

export const UpdateNotificationBanner: React.FC<UpdateBannerProps> = ({
  updateInfo,
  onOpenDetails,
  onApplyUpdate,
  onDismiss,
}) => {
  const [isApplying, setIsApplying] = useState(false);

  const handleUpdate = async () => {
    setIsApplying(true);
    try {
      if (updateInfo.downloadUrl) {
        window.open(updateInfo.downloadUrl, '_blank');
        setIsApplying(false);
        onDismiss();
        return;
      }
      await updateService.applyUpdate();
    } catch {
      window.location.reload();
    }
  };

  return (
    <aside 
      aria-label="Aviso de actualización disponible"
      className="fixed top-3 inset-x-3 sm:inset-x-auto sm:right-4 sm:max-w-md z-[9999] bg-[#0a121d]/95 backdrop-blur-xl border border-cyan-500/50 rounded-2xl p-3 shadow-2xl shadow-cyan-500/20 animate-in slide-in-from-top-4 duration-300 flex items-center justify-between gap-3 text-white"
    >
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 min-w-0">
        <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-cyan-500/20 border border-cyan-500/40 text-[#00e5ff] flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 shadow-sm animate-pulse">
          <Sparkles className="w-4.5 h-4.5" />
        </div>
        <div className="min-w-0">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-[#00e5ff]">
              Actualización lista
            </span>
            <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-slate-400">
              v{updateInfo.latestVersion}
            </span>
          </div>
          <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-200 truncate mt-0.5">
            Nuevas mejoras de navegación y zoom
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={handleUpdate}
          disabled={isApplying}
          className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-[#00e5ff] hover:bg-cyan-400 text-slate-950 font-bold text-xs shadow-md shadow-cyan-500/30 flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-all active:scale-95"
        >
          {isApplying ? (
            <RefreshCw className="w-3.5 h-3.5 animate-spin" />
          ) : (
            <RefreshCw className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          )}
          <span>Actualizar</span>
        </button>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onOpenDetails}
          className="p-1.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 text-slate-400 hover:text-white transition-colors"
          title="Ver novedades"
        >
          <ChevronRight className="w-4 h-4" />
        </button>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onDismiss}
          className="p-1.5 rounded-xl text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-300 transition-colors"
          title="Posponer"
        >
          <X className="w-4 h-4" />
        </button>
      </div>
    </aside>
  );
};

/**
 * IndexedDB storage engine for Bóveda Segura.
 * Stores AES-GCM encrypted binary photos, videos, and application files locally.
 */

import { hexToBuf } from '../crypto/vaultCrypto';

export type VaultItemCategory = 'photo' | 'video' | 'audio' | 'app';

export function detectCategory(filename: string, mimeType: string): VaultItemCategory {
  const lowerName = filename.toLowerCase();
  const lowerMime = (mimeType || '').toLowerCase();

  if (
    lowerMime.startsWith('video/') ||
    /\.(mp4|webm|mkv|mov|avi|flv|m4v|3gp|wmv|ts)$/i.test(lowerName)
  ) {
    return 'video';
  }

  if (
    lowerMime.startsWith('audio/') ||
    /\.(mp3|wav|ogg|m4a|aac|flac|opus|wma|amr|mid|midi)$/i.test(lowerName)
  ) {
    return 'audio';
  }

  if (
    lowerMime.includes('android.package-archive') ||
    lowerMime.includes('x-msdownload') ||
    lowerMime.includes('zip') ||
    lowerMime.includes('x-zip') ||
    lowerMime.includes('octet-stream') ||
    lowerMime.includes('pdf') ||
    /\.(apk|exe|msi|dmg|pkg|deb|rpm|ipa|zip|rar|tar|gz|7z|bin|iso|appimage|jar|pdf|docx?|xlsx?|pptx?|txt)$/i.test(lowerName)
  ) {
    return 'app';
  }

  if (
    lowerMime.startsWith('image/') ||
    /\.(jpg|jpeg|png|gif|webp|bmp|svg|avif|heic|ico)$/i.test(lowerName)
  ) {
    return 'photo';
  }

  // Fallback for document or application packages
  return 'app';
}

export interface EncryptedVaultItem {
  id: string;
  timestamp: number;
  name: string;
  mimeType: string;
  size: number;
  iv: number[];
  data: ArrayBuffer;
  album?: string;
  isFavorite?: boolean;
  category: VaultItemCategory;
  lastModified?: number;
  originalUri?: string;
}

// Backwards compatibility alias
export type EncryptedPhotoRecord = EncryptedVaultItem;

const DB_NAME = 'SecurePhotoVaultDB';
const STORE_NAME = 'encrypted_photos';
const DB_VERSION = 2;

let dbInstance: IDBDatabase | null = null;

export function openVaultDatabase(): Promise<IDBDatabase> {
  if (dbInstance) return Promise.resolve(dbInstance);

  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const request = indexedDB.open(DB_NAME, DB_VERSION);

    request.onerror = () => {
      reject(request.error);
    };

    request.onsuccess = () => {
      dbInstance = request.result;
      resolve(dbInstance);
    };

    request.onupgradeneeded = (event) => {
      const db = (event.target as IDBOpenDBRequest).result;
      if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains(STORE_NAME)) {
        const store = db.createObjectStore(STORE_NAME, { keyPath: 'id' });
        store.createIndex('timestamp', 'timestamp', { unique: false });
      }
    };
  });
}

export async function saveEncryptedItem(record: EncryptedVaultItem): Promise<void> {
  return saveEncryptedItems([record]);
}

export async function saveEncryptedItems(records: EncryptedVaultItem[]): Promise<void> {
  if (!records || records.length === 0) return;
  const db = await openVaultDatabase();
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const transaction = db.transaction([STORE_NAME], 'readwrite');
    const store = transaction.objectStore(STORE_NAME);

    transaction.oncomplete = () => resolve();
    transaction.onerror = () => reject(transaction.error);
    transaction.onabort = () => reject(transaction.error);

    for (const record of records) {
      store.put(record);
    }
  });
}

// Alias for photo saving
export const saveEncryptedPhoto = saveEncryptedItem;

export async function getAllEncryptedItems(): Promise<EncryptedVaultItem[]> {
  const db = await openVaultDatabase();
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const transaction = db.transaction([STORE_NAME], 'readonly');
    const store = transaction.objectStore(STORE_NAME);
    const request = store.getAll();

    request.onsuccess = () => {
      const results: EncryptedVaultItem[] = (request.result || []).map((raw: any) => {
        const name = raw.name || `item_${raw.id}`;
        const mimeType = raw.mimeType || (raw.category === 'video' ? 'video/mp4' : 'image/jpeg');
        const category = raw.category || detectCategory(name, mimeType);

        return {
          id: raw.id,
          timestamp: raw.timestamp || Date.now(),
          name,
          mimeType,
          size: raw.size || (raw.data ? raw.data.byteLength : 0),
          iv: raw.iv,
          data: raw.data,
          album: raw.album || 'General',
          isFavorite: !!raw.isFavorite,
          category,
          lastModified: raw.lastModified,
          originalUri: raw.originalUri,
        };
      });

      resolve(results);
    };
    request.onerror = () => reject(request.error);
  });
}

// Alias for photos
export const getAllEncryptedPhotos = getAllEncryptedItems;

export async function getEncryptedItemById(id: string): Promise<EncryptedVaultItem | null> {
  const db = await openVaultDatabase();
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const transaction = db.transaction([STORE_NAME], 'readonly');
    const store = transaction.objectStore(STORE_NAME);
    const request = store.get(id);

    request.onsuccess = () => {
      const raw = request.result;
      if (!raw) {
        resolve(null);
        return;
      }
      const name = raw.name || `item_${raw.id}`;
      const mimeType = raw.mimeType || 'image/jpeg';
      const category = raw.category || detectCategory(name, mimeType);

      resolve({
        id: raw.id,
        timestamp: raw.timestamp,
        name,
        mimeType,
        size: raw.size || (raw.data ? raw.data.byteLength : 0),
        iv: raw.iv,
        data: raw.data,
        album: raw.album || 'General',
        isFavorite: !!raw.isFavorite,
        category,
      });
    };
    request.onerror = () => reject(request.error);
  });
}

export async function deleteEncryptedItem(id: string): Promise<void> {
  const db = await openVaultDatabase();
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const transaction = db.transaction([STORE_NAME], 'readwrite');
    const store = transaction.objectStore(STORE_NAME);
    const request = store.delete(id);

    request.onsuccess = () => resolve();
    request.onerror = () => reject(request.error);
  });
}

export const deleteEncryptedPhoto = deleteEncryptedItem;

export async function deleteMultipleEncryptedItems(ids: string[]): Promise<void> {
  const db = await openVaultDatabase();
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const transaction = db.transaction([STORE_NAME], 'readwrite');
    const store = transaction.objectStore(STORE_NAME);

    ids.forEach((id) => {
      store.delete(id);
    });

    transaction.oncomplete = () => resolve();
    transaction.onerror = () => reject(transaction.error);
  });
}

export const deleteMultipleEncryptedPhotos = deleteMultipleEncryptedItems;

export async function updateItemMetadata(
  id: string,
  updates: Partial<Omit<EncryptedVaultItem, 'id' | 'data' | 'iv'>>
): Promise<void> {
  const db = await openVaultDatabase();
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const transaction = db.transaction([STORE_NAME], 'readwrite');
    const store = transaction.objectStore(STORE_NAME);
    const request = store.get(id);

    request.onsuccess = () => {
      const existing = request.result;
      if (!existing) {
        reject(new Error('Record not found'));
        return;
      }
      const updated = { ...existing, ...updates };
      const putRequest = store.put(updated);
      putRequest.onsuccess = () => resolve();
      putRequest.onerror = () => reject(putRequest.error);
    };
    request.onerror = () => reject(request.error);
  });
}

export const updatePhotoMetadata = updateItemMetadata;

export async function batchUpdateAlbum(ids: string[], album: string): Promise<void> {
  const db = await openVaultDatabase();
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const transaction = db.transaction([STORE_NAME], 'readwrite');
    const store = transaction.objectStore(STORE_NAME);

    ids.forEach((id) => {
      const getReq = store.get(id);
      getReq.onsuccess = () => {
        if (getReq.result) {
          store.put({ ...getReq.result, album });
        }
      };
    });

    transaction.oncomplete = () => resolve();
    transaction.onerror = () => reject(transaction.error);
  });
}

export async function batchRenameAlbum(
  category: VaultItemCategory,
  oldAlbum: string,
  newAlbum: string
): Promise<void> {
  const db = await openVaultDatabase();
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const transaction = db.transaction([STORE_NAME], 'readwrite');
    const store = transaction.objectStore(STORE_NAME);
    const request = store.openCursor();

    request.onsuccess = (event) => {
      const cursor = (event.target as IDBRequest<IDBCursorWithValue>).result;
      if (cursor) {
        const item: EncryptedVaultItem = cursor.value;
        const currentAlbum = item.album || 'General';
        if (item.category === category && currentAlbum === oldAlbum) {
          cursor.update({ ...item, album: newAlbum });
        }
        cursor.continue();
      }
    };

    transaction.oncomplete = () => resolve();
    transaction.onerror = () => reject(transaction.error);
  });
}

export async function clearVaultDatabase(): Promise<void> {
  const db = await openVaultDatabase();
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const transaction = db.transaction([STORE_NAME], 'readwrite');
    const store = transaction.objectStore(STORE_NAME);
    const request = store.clear();

    request.onsuccess = () => resolve();
    request.onerror = () => reject(request.error);
  });
}

export async function getVaultStats(): Promise<{
  count: number;
  totalBytes: number;
  photoCount: number;
  videoCount: number;
  appCount: number;
}> {
  const items = await getAllEncryptedItems();
  let totalBytes = 0;
  let photoCount = 0;
  let videoCount = 0;
  let appCount = 0;

  items.forEach((item) => {
    totalBytes += item.size || item.data.byteLength || 0;
    if (item.category === 'photo') photoCount++;
    else if (item.category === 'video') videoCount++;
    else appCount++;
  });

  return {
    count: items.length,
    totalBytes,
    photoCount,
    videoCount,
    appCount,
  };
}

/**
 * Fast synchronous binary buffer to Base64 conversion with 32KB chunks to prevent stack limits
 */
export function bufferToBase64(buffer: ArrayBuffer | Uint8Array): string {
  try {
    const bytes = buffer instanceof Uint8Array ? buffer : new Uint8Array(buffer);
    let binary = '';
    const len = bytes.byteLength;
    const chunkSize = 0x8000; // 32KB chunks
    for (let i = 0; i < len; i += chunkSize) {
      binary += String.fromCharCode.apply(null, bytes.subarray(i, Math.min(i + chunkSize, len)) as any);
    }
    return btoa(binary);
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('bufferToBase64 fallback failed:', err);
    return '';
  }
}

/**
 * Creates exportable JSON containing encrypted blobs as Base64 strings.
 * Highly optimized native FileReader-based ArrayBuffer to Base64 conversion with fallback.
 */
async function arrayBufferToBase64Async(buffer: ArrayBuffer | Uint8Array | string | undefined | null): Promise<string> {
  if (!buffer) return '';
  if (typeof buffer === 'string') return buffer;

  const uint8 = buffer instanceof Uint8Array ? buffer : new Uint8Array(buffer);

  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    try {
      const blob = new Blob([uint8 as unknown as BlobPart]);
      const reader = new FileReader();

      // Guard timeout in case FileReader hangs on mobile browser
      const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => {
        try {
          resolve(bufferToBase64(uint8));
        } catch {
          resolve('');
        }
      }, 2500);

      reader.onloadend = () => {
        clearTimeout(timeoutId);
        try {
          const dataUrl = reader.result as string;
          if (!dataUrl) {
            resolve(bufferToBase64(uint8));
            return;
          }
          const commaIndex = dataUrl.indexOf(',');
          const base64 = commaIndex > -1 ? dataUrl.substring(commaIndex + 1) : '';
          resolve(base64);
        } catch (e) {
          resolve(bufferToBase64(uint8));
        }
      };

      reader.onerror = () => {
        clearTimeout(timeoutId);
        resolve(bufferToBase64(uint8));
      };

      reader.readAsDataURL(blob);
    } catch (err) {
      try {
        resolve(bufferToBase64(uint8));
      } catch {
        resolve('');
      }
    }
  });
}

/**
 * Fast native conversion from Base64 to ArrayBuffer without fetch data URL memory limits.
 * Uses chunked decoding for large files to avoid V8 mobile heap exhaustion and atob string limits.
 */
function base64ToArrayBuffer(rawBase64: string): ArrayBuffer {
  if (!rawBase64) return new ArrayBuffer(0);
  try {
    let clean = rawBase64;
    const commaIdx = clean.indexOf(',');
    if (commaIdx !== -1) {
      clean = clean.substring(commaIdx + 1);
    }
    clean = clean.replace(/[\r\n\s]/g, '');

    const padding = clean.endsWith('==') ? 2 : clean.endsWith('=') ? 1 : 0;
    const totalLen = Math.floor((clean.length * 3) / 4) - padding;
    if (totalLen <= 0) return new ArrayBuffer(0);

    const result = new Uint8Array(totalLen);
    const CHUNK_SIZE = 65536; // 64KB multiple of 4
    let destOffset = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < clean.length; i += CHUNK_SIZE) {
      const chunkStr = clean.substring(i, Math.min(i + CHUNK_SIZE, clean.length));
      const binChunk = atob(chunkStr);
      for (let j = 0; j < binChunk.length && destOffset < totalLen; j++) {
        result[destOffset++] = binChunk.charCodeAt(j);
      }
    }
    return result.buffer;
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('Error decoding base64:', err);
    return new ArrayBuffer(0);
  }
}

async function base64ToArrayBufferAsync(base64: string): Promise<ArrayBuffer> {
  return base64ToArrayBuffer(base64);
}

function getSafeFolders(cat: string): string[] {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(`vault_subfolders_${cat}`);
    if (raw) {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
      if (Array.isArray(parsed) && parsed.length > 0) return parsed;
    }
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn(`Error reading subfolders for ${cat}:`, e);
  }
  return ['General'];
}

/**
 * Exports all encrypted items and cryptographic salt to a Blob in streaming chunks
 */
export async function exportEncryptedBackupBlob(
  onProgress?: (current: number, total: number) => void
): Promise<Blob> {
  const db = await openVaultDatabase();
  const blobParts: BlobPart[] = [];

  const saltHex = localStorage.getItem('vault_salt') || '';
  const testIvHex = localStorage.getItem('vault_test_iv') || '';
  const testCipherHex = localStorage.getItem('vault_test_cipher') || '';
  const customFolders = {
    photo: getSafeFolders('photo'),
    video: getSafeFolders('video'),
    audio: getSafeFolders('audio'),
    app: getSafeFolders('app'),
  };

  blobParts.push('{\n');
  blobParts.push('  "version": 3,\n');
  blobParts.push('  "appName": "Bóveda Segura - Fotos, Videos y Apps",\n');
  blobParts.push(`  "exportedAt": ${JSON.stringify(new Date().toISOString())},\n`);
  blobParts.push(`  "saltHex": ${JSON.stringify(saltHex)},\n`);
  blobParts.push(`  "testIvHex": ${JSON.stringify(testIvHex)},\n`);
  blobParts.push(`  "testCipherHex": ${JSON.stringify(testCipherHex)},\n`);
  blobParts.push(`  "customFolders": ${JSON.stringify(customFolders)},\n`);
  blobParts.push('  "items": [\n');

  // Pre-flight key list query (uses very little memory)
  const keys = await new Promise<IDBValidKey[]>((resolve) => {
    const transaction = db.transaction([STORE_NAME], 'readonly');
    const store = transaction.objectStore(STORE_NAME);
    const req = store.getAllKeys();
    req.onsuccess = () => resolve(req.result || []);
    req.onerror = () => resolve([]);
  });

  let firstItem = true;
  const totalCount = keys.length;

  // Process and stringify each item sequentially, opening fresh small transactions
  for (let i = 0; i < totalCount; i++) {
    const id = keys[i];

    if (onProgress) {
      onProgress(i + 1, totalCount);
    }

    const raw = await new Promise<any>((resolve) => {
      const transaction = db.transaction([STORE_NAME], 'readonly');
      const store = transaction.objectStore(STORE_NAME);
      const req = store.get(id);
      req.onsuccess = () => resolve(req.result);
      req.onerror = () => resolve(null);
    });

    if (!raw) continue;

    try {
      const name = raw.name || `item_${raw.id}`;
      const mimeType = raw.mimeType || (raw.category === 'video' ? 'video/mp4' : 'image/jpeg');
      const category = raw.category || detectCategory(name, mimeType);

      // Memory-efficient native base64 conversion
      const base64Data = raw.data ? await arrayBufferToBase64Async(raw.data) : '';
      
      // IMMEDIATELY dereference raw.data to free binary memory from V8 heap
      raw.data = null;

      const itemObj = {
        id: raw.id,
        timestamp: raw.timestamp || Date.now(),
        name,
        mimeType,
        size: raw.size || 0,
        iv: Array.isArray(raw.iv) ? raw.iv : (raw.iv ? Array.from(raw.iv) : []),
        base64Data,
        album: raw.album || 'General',
        isFavorite: !!raw.isFavorite,
        category,
      };

      if (!firstItem) {
        blobParts.push(',\n');
      }
      blobParts.push('    ' + JSON.stringify(itemObj));
      firstItem = false;
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Error exporting item details:', err);
    }

    // Force full yield to event loop and Garbage Collection between files
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 30));
  }

  blobParts.push('\n  ]\n}');

  return new Blob(blobParts, { type: 'application/json' });
}

export async function exportEncryptedBackup(): Promise<string> {
  const blob = await exportEncryptedBackupBlob();
  return await blob.text();
}

/**
 * Attempts to repair truncated JSON files if missing closing brackets
 */
export function parseWithAutoRepair(jsonStr: string): any {
  let trimmed = jsonStr.trim();
  if (!trimmed) {
    throw new Error('El archivo de copia de seguridad está completamente vacío');
  }

  // Strip Byte Order Mark (BOM) if present
  if (trimmed.charCodeAt(0) === 0xFEFF) {
    trimmed = trimmed.slice(1);
  }
  // Strip trailing null characters and spaces
  trimmed = trimmed.replace(/\0+$/, '').trim();

  // Try direct parse first in case it was just padded
  try {
    return JSON.parse(trimmed);
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('Initial parse in auto-repair failed, attempting structural repair:', err);
  }

  // Bracket balancing scan ignoring string literals and escapes
  let inString = false;
  let escape = false;
  const stack: ('{' | '[')[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < trimmed.length; i++) {
    const char = trimmed[i];
    if (escape) {
      escape = false;
      continue;
    }
    if (char === '\\') {
      escape = true;
      continue;
    }
    if (char === '"') {
      inString = !inString;
      continue;
    }
    if (!inString) {
      if (char === '{') {
        stack.push('{');
      } else if (char === '[') {
        stack.push('[');
      } else if (char === '}') {
        if (stack[stack.length - 1] === '{') {
          stack.pop();
        }
      } else if (char === ']') {
        if (stack[stack.length - 1] === '[') {
          stack.pop();
        }
      }
    }
  }

  // Find the last complete item ending with '}'
  const lastItemEnd = trimmed.lastIndexOf('}');
  if (lastItemEnd > 0) {
    let repaired = trimmed.substring(0, lastItemEnd + 1).trim();
    if (repaired.endsWith(',')) {
      repaired = repaired.slice(0, -1).trim();
    }

    // Now balance the stack for this repaired prefix
    const prefixStack: ('{' | '[')[] = [];
    inString = false;
    escape = false;
    for (let i = 0; i < repaired.length; i++) {
      const char = repaired[i];
      if (escape) { escape = false; continue; }
      if (char === '\\') { escape = true; continue; }
      if (char === '"') { inString = !inString; continue; }
      if (!inString) {
        if (char === '{') prefixStack.push('{');
        else if (char === '[') prefixStack.push('[');
        else if (char === '}') { if (prefixStack[prefixStack.length - 1] === '{') prefixStack.pop(); }
        else if (char === ']') { if (prefixStack[prefixStack.length - 1] === '[') prefixStack.pop(); }
      }
    }

    // Close open structures in reverse order
    while (prefixStack.length > 0) {
      const openToken = prefixStack.pop();
      if (openToken === '{') {
        repaired += '\n}';
      } else if (openToken === '[') {
        repaired += '\n  ]';
      }
    }

    try {
      return JSON.parse(repaired);
    } catch (err2: any) {
      console.error('Failed auto-repaired JSON parse:', err2);
      throw new Error(`El archivo de copia de seguridad está dañado: ${err2?.message || 'Unexpected end of JSON input'}`);
    }
  }

  throw new Error('El archivo de copia de seguridad está dañado o incompleto (Unexpected end of JSON input)');
}

export interface BackupHeaderInfo {
  saltHex?: string;
  testIvHex?: string;
  testCipherHex?: string;
  customFolders?: any;
  hasItems: boolean;
}

/**
 * Rapidly reads only the first ~512KB of the backup file to inspect header and cryptographic tokens,
 * without loading the rest of a multi-hundred-megabyte file into memory.
 */
export async function readBackupHeaderFromBlob(blob: Blob): Promise<BackupHeaderInfo> {
  const headerSlice = blob.slice(0, Math.min(blob.size, 512 * 1024));
  const headerText = await headerSlice.text();

  const saltMatch = headerText.match(/"saltHex"\s*:\s*"([a-fA-F0-9]+)"/);
  const ivMatch = headerText.match(/"testIvHex"\s*:\s*"([a-fA-F0-9]+)"/);
  const cipherMatch = headerText.match(/"testCipherHex"\s*:\s*"([a-fA-F0-9]+)"/);

  let customFolders: any = null;
  const foldersMatch = headerText.match(/"customFolders"\s*:\s*(\{.+?\})/s);
  if (foldersMatch) {
    try {
      customFolders = JSON.parse(foldersMatch[1]);
    } catch {}
  }

  const hasItems = /"items"\s*:\s*\[|"photos"\s*:\s*\[|"data"\s*:\s*\[|"records"\s*:\s*\[|^\s*\[/m.test(headerText);

  return {
    saltHex: saltMatch?.[1],
    testIvHex: ivMatch?.[1],
    testCipherHex: cipherMatch?.[1],
    customFolders,
    hasItems,
  };
}

async function saveItemsBatch(db: IDBDatabase, records: EncryptedVaultItem[]): Promise<void> {
  if (records.length === 0) return;
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    try {
      const tx = db.transaction([STORE_NAME], 'readwrite');
      const store = tx.objectStore(STORE_NAME);
      for (const rec of records) {
        store.put(rec);
      }
      tx.oncomplete = () => resolve();
      tx.onerror = () => reject(tx.error || new Error('Error al escribir registros en IndexedDB'));
      tx.onabort = () => reject(new Error('Transacción abortada'));
    } catch (err) {
      reject(err);
    }
  });
}

/**
 * Streams and restores encrypted items directly from a Blob or File of ANY size (e.g. 548MB+)
 * using a memory-constant chunked JSON object parser. It processes items one-by-one, saves
 * each to IndexedDB, and releases memory immediately, avoiding V8 string length limits and mobile crashes.
 */
export async function importEncryptedBackupBlobStream(
  blob: Blob,
  onProgress?: (loadedBytes: number, totalBytes: number, itemCount: number) => void,
  overwriteCryptographicTokens: boolean = true
): Promise<{ importedCount: number; saltHex?: string; testIvHex?: string; testCipherHex?: string }> {
  // First, verify header tokens if present
  const header = await readBackupHeaderFromBlob(blob);

  if (header.saltHex && header.testIvHex && header.testCipherHex) {
    if (overwriteCryptographicTokens || !localStorage.getItem('vault_salt')) {
      localStorage.setItem('vault_salt', header.saltHex);
      localStorage.setItem('vault_test_iv', header.testIvHex);
      localStorage.setItem('vault_test_cipher', header.testCipherHex);
    }
  }

  if (header.customFolders) {
    try {
      if (Array.isArray(header.customFolders.photo)) localStorage.setItem('vault_subfolders_photo', JSON.stringify(header.customFolders.photo));
      if (Array.isArray(header.customFolders.video)) localStorage.setItem('vault_subfolders_video', JSON.stringify(header.customFolders.video));
      if (Array.isArray(header.customFolders.audio)) localStorage.setItem('vault_subfolders_audio', JSON.stringify(header.customFolders.audio));
      if (Array.isArray(header.customFolders.app)) localStorage.setItem('vault_subfolders_app', JSON.stringify(header.customFolders.app));
    } catch {}
  }

  const db = await openVaultDatabase();
  let count = 0;
  const totalBytes = blob.size;
  const CHUNK_SIZE = 1024 * 1024 * 4; // 4MB stream slices for high throughput
  let offset = 0;

  let buffer = '';
  let inArray = false;
  let depth = 0;
  let inString = false;
  let escape = false;
  let itemStart = -1;
  let cursor = 0;

  let pendingBatch: EncryptedVaultItem[] = [];
  const decoder = new TextDecoder('utf-8', { fatal: false });

  const parseAndQueueItem = (item: any) => {
    let rawBase64 =
      item.base64Data ||
      item.base64 ||
      item.content ||
      item.dataUrl ||
      item.data_url ||
      item.base64_data ||
      item.fileData ||
      item.file_data ||
      '';
    if (!rawBase64 && typeof item.data === 'string') {
      rawBase64 = item.data;
    }

    let dataBuffer: ArrayBuffer;
    if (item.data instanceof ArrayBuffer) {
      dataBuffer = item.data;
    } else if (rawBase64) {
      dataBuffer = base64ToArrayBuffer(rawBase64);
    } else if (Array.isArray(item.data)) {
      dataBuffer = new Uint8Array(item.data).buffer;
    } else if (item.data && typeof item.data === 'object') {
      if (item.data.type === 'Buffer' && Array.isArray(item.data.data)) {
        dataBuffer = new Uint8Array(item.data.data).buffer;
      } else {
        const values = Object.values(item.data).map(Number);
        dataBuffer = new Uint8Array(values).buffer;
      }
    } else {
      dataBuffer = new ArrayBuffer(0);
    }

    // Auto-generate safe fallback ID and IV
    const id = item.id || `item_${Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 11)}_${Date.now()}`;
    let iv: number[] = [];
    if (Array.isArray(item.iv)) {
      iv = item.iv;
    } else if (item.iv && typeof item.iv === 'object') {
      iv = Object.values(item.iv).map(Number);
    } else if (typeof item.iv === 'string') {
      if (/^[0-9a-fA-F]+$/.test(item.iv)) {
        iv = Array.from(hexToBuf(item.iv));
      } else {
        try {
          const bin = atob(item.iv);
          iv = Array.from(bin).map((c) => c.charCodeAt(0));
        } catch {}
      }
    }

    const name = item.name || item.fileName || item.filename || item.title || 'archivo_restaurado';
    const mimeType = item.mimeType || item.type || (item.category === 'video' ? 'video/mp4' : 'image/jpeg');
    const category: VaultItemCategory = item.category || detectCategory(name, mimeType);

    const record: EncryptedVaultItem = {
      id,
      timestamp: item.timestamp || Date.now(),
      name,
      mimeType,
      size: item.size || dataBuffer.byteLength,
      iv,
      data: dataBuffer,
      album: item.album || item.folder || item.subfolder || 'General',
      isFavorite: !!item.isFavorite,
      category,
    };

    pendingBatch.push(record);
    count++;
  };

  while (offset < totalBytes || cursor < buffer.length) {
    // Read next slice into buffer if needed
    if (offset < totalBytes && buffer.length - cursor < CHUNK_SIZE) {
      const nextOffset = Math.min(offset + CHUNK_SIZE, totalBytes);
      const slice = blob.slice(offset, nextOffset);
      const chunkText = decoder.decode(await slice.arrayBuffer(), { stream: nextOffset < totalBytes });
      offset = nextOffset;
      buffer += chunkText;
    }

    if (!inArray) {
      const arrayKeyMatches = [
        buffer.indexOf('"items":'),
        buffer.indexOf('"photos":'),
        buffer.indexOf('"files":'),
        buffer.indexOf('"archivos":'),
        buffer.indexOf('"data":'),
        buffer.indexOf('"records":'),
        buffer.indexOf('"media":'),
        buffer.indexOf('"elements":'),
        buffer.indexOf('"elementos":'),
      ].filter((idx) => idx !== -1);

      if (arrayKeyMatches.length > 0) {
        const earliestKeyIdx = Math.min(...arrayKeyMatches);
        const arrIdx = buffer.indexOf('[', earliestKeyIdx);
        if (arrIdx !== -1) {
          buffer = buffer.substring(arrIdx + 1);
          cursor = 0;
          inArray = true;
        } else {
          if (offset >= totalBytes) break;
          cursor = buffer.length;
        }
      } else {
        const arrIdx = buffer.indexOf('[');
        if (arrIdx !== -1 && !buffer.slice(0, arrIdx).includes('{')) {
          buffer = buffer.substring(arrIdx + 1);
          cursor = 0;
          inArray = true;
        } else {
          if (offset >= totalBytes) break;
          cursor = buffer.length;
        }
      }
    }

    if (inArray) {
      while (cursor < buffer.length) {
        const c = buffer[cursor];
        if (escape) {
          escape = false;
          cursor++;
          continue;
        }
        if (c === '\\') {
          escape = true;
          cursor++;
          continue;
        }
        if (c === '"') {
          inString = !inString;
          cursor++;
          continue;
        }
        if (!inString) {
          if (c === '{') {
            if (depth === 0) itemStart = cursor;
            depth++;
          } else if (c === '}') {
            depth--;
            if (depth === 0 && itemStart !== -1) {
              const itemJson = buffer.substring(itemStart, cursor + 1);
              try {
                const item = JSON.parse(itemJson);
                parseAndQueueItem(item);

                if (pendingBatch.length >= 10) {
                  await saveItemsBatch(db, pendingBatch);
                  pendingBatch = [];
                }
              } catch (parseErr) {
                console.warn('Skipping malformed item in stream:', parseErr);
              }

              // Discard parsed object from buffer immediately to free memory
              buffer = buffer.substring(cursor + 1);
              cursor = 0;
              itemStart = -1;
              continue;
            }
          } else if (c === ']' && depth === 0) {
            // End of items array reached
            buffer = '';
            offset = totalBytes;
            break;
          }
        }
        cursor++;
      }
    }

    if (onProgress) {
      onProgress(offset, totalBytes, count);
    }

    // If offset has reached totalBytes and no item is currently being read, we can finish
    if (offset >= totalBytes && itemStart === -1 && (!inArray || cursor >= buffer.length)) {
      break;
    }

    // Cooperative yield to keep UI responsive
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 0));
  }

  // Save any remaining items in pendingBatch
  if (pendingBatch.length > 0) {
    await saveItemsBatch(db, pendingBatch);
    pendingBatch = [];
  }

  // Fallback for smaller files or unconventional JSON layout if stream found 0 items
  if (count === 0 && blob.size < 30 * 1024 * 1024) {
    try {
      const fullText = await blob.text();
      const parsed = parseWithAutoRepair(fullText);
      const itemsList =
        (Array.isArray(parsed) && parsed) ||
        (Array.isArray(parsed.items) && parsed.items) ||
        (Array.isArray(parsed.photos) && parsed.photos) ||
        (Array.isArray(parsed.files) && parsed.files) ||
        (Array.isArray(parsed.archivos) && parsed.archivos) ||
        (Array.isArray(parsed.data) && parsed.data) ||
        (Array.isArray(parsed.records) && parsed.records) ||
        (Array.isArray(parsed.media) && parsed.media) ||
        [];

      if (itemsList.length > 0) {
        for (const it of itemsList) {
          parseAndQueueItem(it);
          if (pendingBatch.length >= 10) {
            await saveItemsBatch(db, pendingBatch);
            pendingBatch = [];
          }
        }
        if (pendingBatch.length > 0) {
          await saveItemsBatch(db, pendingBatch);
          pendingBatch = [];
        }
      }
    } catch (fbErr) {
      console.warn('Fallback full parse attempt error:', fbErr);
    }
  }

  if (onProgress) {
    onProgress(totalBytes, totalBytes, count);
  }

  return {
    importedCount: count,
    saltHex: header.saltHex,
    testIvHex: header.testIvHex,
    testCipherHex: header.testCipherHex,
  };
}

/**
 * Restores items and optional cryptographic tokens from a backup file (compatibility wrapper)
 */
export async function importEncryptedBackup(
  jsonContent: string,
  onProgress?: (current: number, total: number) => void,
  overwriteCryptographicTokens: boolean = false
): Promise<{ importedCount: number }> {
  const blob = new Blob([jsonContent], { type: 'application/json' });
  return importEncryptedBackupBlobStream(
    blob,
    (loaded, total, count) => {
      if (onProgress) onProgress(count, count);
    },
    overwriteCryptographicTokens
  );
}

/**
 * Cryptographic engine for Bóveda Segura using Web Crypto API.
 * Uses PBKDF2 (100,000 rounds, SHA-256) + AES-GCM (256-bit).
 */

export const TEST_VERIFICATION_STRING = 'VAULT_VERIFY_KEY_OK';

export function bufToHex(buffer: Uint8Array): string {
  return Array.from(buffer)
    .map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0'))
    .join('');
}

export function hexToBuf(hex: string): Uint8Array {
  const matches = hex.match(/.{1,2}/g);
  if (!matches) return new Uint8Array(0);
  return new Uint8Array(matches.map((byte) => parseInt(byte, 16)));
}

/**
 * Derives a 256-bit AES-GCM key from user PIN and cryptographic salt
 */
export async function deriveKeyFromPin(pin: string, salt: Uint8Array): Promise<CryptoKey> {
  const enc = new TextEncoder();
  const keyMaterial = await window.crypto.subtle.importKey(
    'raw',
    enc.encode(pin),
    { name: 'PBKDF2' },
    false,
    ['deriveKey']
  );

  return await window.crypto.subtle.deriveKey(
    {
      name: 'PBKDF2',
      salt: salt as BufferSource,
      iterations: 100000,
      hash: 'SHA-256',
    },
    keyMaterial,
    { name: 'AES-GCM', length: 256 },
    true,
    ['encrypt', 'decrypt']
  );
}

/**
 * Exports raw key bytes for backup or recovery escrow wrapping
 */
export async function exportMasterKeyRaw(key: CryptoKey): Promise<ArrayBuffer> {
  return await window.crypto.subtle.exportKey('raw', key);
}

/**
 * Imports raw 256-bit AES-GCM master key
 */
export async function importMasterKeyRaw(rawBytes: ArrayBuffer): Promise<CryptoKey> {
  return await window.crypto.subtle.importKey(
    'raw',
    rawBytes,
    { name: 'AES-GCM' },
    true,
    ['encrypt', 'decrypt']
  );
}

/**
 * Derives a key for wrapping the master key for email recovery escrow
 */
export async function deriveRecoveryWrapKey(secret: string, salt: Uint8Array): Promise<CryptoKey> {
  const enc = new TextEncoder();
  const keyMaterial = await window.crypto.subtle.importKey(
    'raw',
    enc.encode(secret),
    { name: 'PBKDF2' },
    false,
    ['deriveKey']
  );

  return await window.crypto.subtle.deriveKey(
    {
      name: 'PBKDF2',
      salt: salt as BufferSource,
      iterations: 50000,
      hash: 'SHA-256',
    },
    keyMaterial,
    { name: 'AES-GCM', length: 256 },
    false,
    ['encrypt', 'decrypt']
  );
}

/**
 * Creates random 16-byte salt
 */
export function generateSalt(): Uint8Array {
  return window.crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(16));
}

/**
 * Creates random 12-byte IV for AES-GCM
 */
export function generateIV(): Uint8Array {
  return window.crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(12));
}

/**
 * Encrypts an ArrayBuffer payload using AES-GCM
 */
export async function encryptBuffer(
  buffer: ArrayBuffer,
  key: CryptoKey
): Promise<{ iv: number[]; data: ArrayBuffer }> {
  const iv = generateIV();
  const encrypted = await window.crypto.subtle.encrypt(
    { name: 'AES-GCM', iv: iv as BufferSource },
    key,
    buffer
  );

  return {
    iv: Array.from(iv),
    data: encrypted,
  };
}

/**
 * Decrypts an ArrayBuffer using AES-GCM and stored IV
 */
export async function decryptBuffer(
  encryptedData: ArrayBuffer,
  ivArray: number[],
  key: CryptoKey
): Promise<ArrayBuffer> {
  const iv = new Uint8Array(ivArray);
  return await window.crypto.subtle.decrypt(
    { name: 'AES-GCM', iv: iv as BufferSource },
    key,
    encryptedData
  );
}

/**
 * Creates verification token for verifying PIN validity during unlock
 */
export async function createVerificationPayload(key: CryptoKey): Promise<{ ivHex: string; cipherHex: string }> {
  const iv = generateIV();
  const testBytes = new TextEncoder().encode(TEST_VERIFICATION_STRING);
  const encrypted = await window.crypto.subtle.encrypt(
    { name: 'AES-GCM', iv: iv as BufferSource },
    key,
    testBytes
  );

  return {
    ivHex: bufToHex(iv),
    cipherHex: bufToHex(new Uint8Array(encrypted)),
  };
}

/**
 * Verifies if a given CryptoKey correctly decrypts the verification token
 */
export async function verifyKey(
  key: CryptoKey,
  ivHex: string,
  cipherHex: string
): Promise<boolean> {
  try {
    const iv = hexToBuf(ivHex);
    const cipher = hexToBuf(cipherHex);
    const decrypted = await window.crypto.subtle.decrypt(
      { name: 'AES-GCM', iv: iv as BufferSource },
      key,
      cipher as BufferSource
    );
    const str = new TextDecoder().decode(decrypted);
    return str === TEST_VERIFICATION_STRING;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

/**
 * Checks if a given buffer starts with known magic bytes of unencrypted media (images, videos, audio, PDF, archives, docs)
 */
export function checkIsUnencryptedMedia(buffer: ArrayBuffer | Uint8Array | null | undefined): boolean {
  if (!buffer) return false;
  const byteLen = buffer instanceof Uint8Array ? buffer.byteLength : buffer.byteLength;
  if (byteLen < 2) return false;

  const bytes = buffer instanceof Uint8Array ? buffer : new Uint8Array(buffer, 0, Math.min(byteLen, 32));

  // JPEG: FF D8 FF
  if (bytes.length >= 3 && bytes[0] === 0xff && bytes[1] === 0xd8 && bytes[2] === 0xff) return true;
  // PNG: 89 50 4E 47
  if (bytes.length >= 4 && bytes[0] === 0x89 && bytes[1] === 0x50 && bytes[2] === 0x4e && bytes[3] === 0x47) return true;
  // GIF: 47 49 46 38 ('GIF8')
  if (bytes.length >= 4 && bytes[0] === 0x47 && bytes[1] === 0x49 && bytes[2] === 0x46 && bytes[3] === 0x38) return true;
  // WEBP / RIFF (WAV, AVI, WEBP): 52 49 46 46 ('RIFF')
  if (bytes.length >= 4 && bytes[0] === 0x52 && bytes[1] === 0x49 && bytes[2] === 0x46 && bytes[3] === 0x46) return true;
  // MP4 / MOV / 3GP / M4A / QuickTime: 'ftyp', 'moov', 'mdat'
  if (bytes.length >= 8) {
    const s = String.fromCharCode(bytes[4], bytes[5], bytes[6], bytes[7]);
    if (s === 'ftyp' || s === 'moov' || s === 'mdat' || s === 'free' || s === 'skip' || s === 'wide') return true;
  }
  // PDF: 25 50 44 46 ('%PDF')
  if (bytes.length >= 4 && bytes[0] === 0x25 && bytes[1] === 0x50 && bytes[2] === 0x44 && bytes[3] === 0x46) return true;
  // MP3: 'ID3' (49 44 33) or sync word FF FB / FF F3 / FF F2
  if (bytes.length >= 3 && bytes[0] === 0x49 && bytes[1] === 0x44 && bytes[2] === 0x33) return true;
  if (bytes.length >= 2 && bytes[0] === 0xff && (bytes[1] === 0xfb || bytes[1] === 0xf3 || bytes[1] === 0xf2)) return true;
  // WebM / Matroska / MKV: 1A 45 DF A3
  if (bytes.length >= 4 && bytes[0] === 0x1a && bytes[1] === 0x45 && bytes[2] === 0xdf && bytes[3] === 0xa3) return true;
  // ZIP / APK / DOCX / XLSX / PPTX / JAR: 50 4B 03 04 / 50 4B 05 06 / 50 4B 07 08 ('PK..')
  if (bytes.length >= 4 && bytes[0] === 0x50 && bytes[1] === 0x4b && (bytes[2] === 0x03 || bytes[2] === 0x05 || bytes[2] === 0x07)) return true;
  // RAR: 52 61 72 21 ('Rar!')
  if (bytes.length >= 4 && bytes[0] === 0x52 && bytes[1] === 0x61 && bytes[2] === 0x72 && bytes[3] === 0x21) return true;
  // 7Z: 37 7A BC AF 27 1C ('7z..')
  if (bytes.length >= 6 && bytes[0] === 0x37 && bytes[1] === 0x7a && bytes[2] === 0xbc && bytes[3] === 0xaf) return true;
  // GZIP: 1F 8B
  if (bytes.length >= 2 && bytes[0] === 0x1f && bytes[1] === 0x8b) return true;
  // OGG: 4F 67 67 53 ('OggS')
  if (bytes.length >= 4 && bytes[0] === 0x4f && bytes[1] === 0x67 && bytes[2] === 0x67 && bytes[3] === 0x53) return true;
  // FLAC: 66 4C 61 43 ('fLaC')
  if (bytes.length >= 4 && bytes[0] === 0x66 && bytes[1] === 0x4c && bytes[2] === 0x61 && bytes[3] === 0x43) return true;
  // BMP: 42 4D ('BM')
  if (bytes.length >= 2 && bytes[0] === 0x42 && bytes[1] === 0x4d) return true;
  // TIFF: 49 49 2A 00 (little endian) or 4D 4D 00 2A (big endian)
  if (bytes.length >= 4 && ((bytes[0] === 0x49 && bytes[1] === 0x49 && bytes[2] === 0x2a && bytes[3] === 0x00) || (bytes[0] === 0x4d && bytes[1] === 0x4d && bytes[2] === 0x00 && bytes[3] === 0x2a))) return true;

  // JSON or XML text
  if (bytes.length >= 1 && (bytes[0] === 0x7b || bytes[0] === 0x5b || bytes[0] === 0x3c)) return true;

  return false;
}



================================================================================
FIN DE ARCHIVO: src/crypto/vaultCrypto.ts
================================================================================
